| Bundle SHA-256 | `168d15a4945ea4ef093da3040fb7bd441ad1ec77ef4143379fbd1a6d318c4fbd` — `bundle.json`, independent `trusted-pins.json` |
| Contract SHA-256 | `28d615baa4b11c72c67f097e25025817f19a18e8abb3c22a6207e7172cda82e6` — `task-contract.json`, independent `trusted-pins.json` |
| Source SHA-256 | `8e13963d42f2b87eb36756e9124922e24bc91ea9ef20af81ff15e9334dfd3f0b` — exact `task-source.json` bytes |
| Runtime image ID | `sha256:3d32c18960e2cd4340c95b9cb3042581be0e2392959e6428fc06a37de334981d` — `build-evidence/image-id.txt`, `build-evidence/image-inspect.json`, actual `evidence/inspect-01.json` |

The local image is `FROM scratch` with one diagnostic marker and no volumes.
`build-evidence/image-inspect.json` records a builder/default PATH environment;
the actual scorer's exact three-variable environment overrides it. The complete
save is `build-evidence/image.tar`; `build-evidence/image-save-members.json`
recomputes every member digest, including the 855-byte image-config blob whose
SHA-256 is the runtime image ID. No registry/official provenance is claimed.
`closure/sh` is the actually installed static BusyBox, SHA-256
`b3c1009e1b5c927e537487c80639cdf404f69e3eb49371d9be5d841672be3ff9`;
actual `file`, `readelf -l` and package output are in `build-evidence/commands.json`, with copyright
saved separately. The scorer graph has three reachable pinned files, 2194251 bytes;
it does not claim a complete closure for any existing dataset.

The task is locally authored addition, 19+23. `closure/expected.txt` is `42\n`,
SHA-256 `084c799cd551dd1d8d5c5f9a5d593b2e931f5e36122ee5c793c1d08a19839cc0`,
and is a sealed dependency outside actor state. The controlled submission is `41\n`;
its actual export SHA-256 is
`040316eca5e77dbb2212c1efe8b81cb23bc67ce0ac8cb5c9d902d98bd45ddfa1`.
The authoritative control-only contract permits only non-executable `answer.txt`,
max_bytes=16, max_inodes=2. This provisions export/private-input instances for this
synthetic task alone; it does not satisfy dataset-wide E1/E2 or official equivalence.

`evidence/launch.json` inventories actor files `answer.txt` and `unselected.txt`,
both mode `0o444`, UID 1026; no private expected file enters actor state. No actor is
launched. Same-UID platform preparation and read-only source files do not establish
race-proof quiescence or exclusion of an external same-UID writer. The driver copies
the fixture into external `platform/scorer/{bundle.json,closure}` and
`platform/contracts/task.json`; pins remain outside the actor. No `runs/` is touched.

## Actual inspection, rechecks and verdict

`evidence/docker-calls.json` is an ordered capture of **actual** `container.docker`
returns using `sys.setprofile`, not replacement functions or a Docker double:
create → inspect → start --attach → inspect → rm --force. Exact argv/stdout/stderr
are additionally in `evidence/docker-00.json` through `evidence/docker-04.json`.

`evidence/inspect-01.json` is the raw stopped-instance inspection:
`State.Status="created"`, `Running=false`, pinned Image as above,
`NetworkMode="none"`, `ReadonlyRootfs=true`, `CapDrop=["ALL"]`,
`Env=["PATH=/control-scorer","HOME=/tmp","TMPDIR=/tmp"]`.
The two actual binds both have `Type="bind"`, `RW=false`, `Propagation="rprivate"`:

| Destination | Actual source |
| --- | --- |
| `/control-input` | `/tmp/hg-pristine-sfa9udgm/input` |
| `/control-scorer` | `/tmp/hg-wpristine-runtime/w-pristine-control-20261014T225600Z-7cbd8f16/platform/scorer/closure` |

`evidence/projection-rechecks.json` captures the original production calls and
their returns. After the inspection at monotonic_ns=6541252297577976 (see raw
`evidence/docker-01.json` for the authoritative timestamp), `verify_scorer_files` returns
`files=3, bytes=2194251` at 6541252310673567; `verify_state_export` returns normally
at 6541252319024541. The artifact inventory contains only `answer.txt`, bytes=3,
mode=`0o600`, UID=1026, nlink=1, matching the receipt hash. The final runtime check
returns `scope="inspected_runtime_contract"` at 6541252325913206, before the start
return at 6541252615057135. Both original projections were rechecked; a null return
from `verify_state_export` is its normal success return, corroborated by the later
successful runtime receipt and actual start. These observations do not attest
that bytes cannot change after the recheck.

`evidence/verdict.json` records executed and declared class `pristine-offline`, with
all four native evidence keys: bundle/export/runtime/cleanup. Export has bytes=3,
inodes=2, limits={max_bytes:16,max_inodes:2}, selected task, contract/source hashes
and the exact generated runtime/export ID. Raw `passed=false`, `score=0.0`,
`detail="sealed scorer exit 1"` is preserved **without treating it as measurement**.

## Real failure, audited counts and production defects

`evidence/docker-02.json` records `docker start --attach` returncode=1, stdout="",
and exact stderr:

```text
/control-scorer/check.sh: line 5: can't open /control-input/answer.txt: Permission denied
```

No `completed_check=fail` or `completed_check=pass` marker was emitted. The scorer
did read its private expected dependency first, but failed before comparing answers.
`evidence/inspect-03.json`: `Status="exited"`, `Running=false`, `ExitCode=1`,
`OOMKilled=false`, `Error=""`, StartedAt=`2026-10-14T22:51:55.278462757Z`,
FinishedAt=`2026-10-14T22:51:55.431904266Z`. Normal process exit is not evidence
that the scoring predicate completed.

1. **Unreadable platform export:** `eval/admission/artifact_admission.py:280,289,292`
   creates the projection directories mode 0700 and files 0600, owned by the calling
   user. Observed exported file ownership/mode is UID 1026/0600. The created image
   has `Config.User=""` (default container root), and
   `eval/verification/pristine.py:67-82` drops all capabilities without aligning
   the container user to export ownership. `evidence/inspect-01.json` corroborates those
   actual settings. On this host it prevents reading the projection. Retained
   negative-probe export directories also actually stat as UID 1026/mode 0700
   (`evidence/host-observations.json`). No chmod/chown/user/capability workaround was used.
2. **Invalid scorer exit becomes numeric zero:**
   `eval/verification/pristine.py:100-108` checks Docker state/OOM/error and maps
   every normal nonzero exit to score 0. This shell initialization failure passes
   that condition. For the generic exit-status protocol, scorer initialization
   and a completed failing check are indistinguishable; this authored control's
   completion marker exposes the difference. No production code was changed.
3. **Stopped result not retained natively:** pristine.py:97-103 reads stopped
   State but does not put it into `scorer_isolation_evidence`; :115-118 retains
   only cleanup name/removal Boolean. Raw inspection/removal evidence exists here
   because the external observer saved the real outputs, not because the native
   verdict contains a stopped-result field.

`evidence/measurement-audit.json`, derived by the recorded audit command from
unchanged raw artifacts, is the measurement decision:

| Control coverage / measurement | Value |
| --- | --- |
| eligible / selected tasks / expected trials | `1 / 1 / 1` |
| completed answer checks / genuine measured zeros | `0 / 0` |
| measured / unmeasured / invalid trials | `0 / 0 / 1` (disjoint counts; invalid means no valid measurement) |
| publishable score | `null` |
| raw native verdict score | `0.0` (invalid for this metric) |
| formal lifecycle / production_authorized | `null / false` |

These disjoint counts follow `eval/policy/diagnostic_policy.py:108-114`: scorer
failures must be invalid or unmeasured; a measured zero requires a successful
measurement contract. The initial `evidence/audited-control-receipt.json` treated invalid
as a subset of unmeasured (0/1/1); it is preserved as a superseded provisional
classification. The final `evidence/measurement-audit.json` partitions the single trial.

The executed driver's first count inference used the numeric verdict and therefore
wrote `measured_trials=1, unmeasured_trials=0, invalid_trials=0` to the original
`evidence/control-receipt.json`. That artifact and `evidence/driver-executed.py` remain unchanged and
are explicitly superseded by the audit, never republished as valid measurement.
The current fixture driver requires the completion marker for future counts; it
was not rerun. No staged pilot receipt or curve score was published.

## Refusals and cleanup, with raw outputs

Three separate **real stopped-instance admission** probes (not task trials) create
actual containers differing from the valid launch in image, network, or extra bind.
Each uses a fresh export with its own run ID and the original production
`require_artifact_runtime` at `artifact_admission.py:378-436`. No inspected JSON is
mutated. These checks do not rerun the production dispatcher or start the scorer.

| Probe / artifacts under `evidence/` | Actual unexpected observation | Actual refusal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `refusal-image-{inspect,result,commands,export}.json` | `Image="sha256:0dd364ba7e10242f07755449e3a3d0e35f9efd987952737b90def6709ab0c5ce"` from `evidence/alternate-image-inspect.json`, different from the pin | `AdmissionError: trusted artifact runtime boundary mismatch` |
| `refusal-network-{inspect,result,commands,export}.json` | `NetworkMode="bridge"` | `AdmissionError: trusted artifact runtime boundary mismatch` |
| `refusal-mount-{inspect,result,commands,export}.json` | third read-only bind at `/unexpected` | `AdmissionError: artifact-only read-only mounts required` |

`evidence/refusal-counts.json` and `evidence/refusals-summary.json`: controls=3, refused=3, started=0,
scored=0, removed=3. Every result has score=null, removed=true, and post-removal
inspect returncode=1. Each commands file contains the real create, inspect, remove
and absent-object inspection outputs. Refusal source is :399-412 (image/network)
and :426-436 (binds). The compliant main instance was **not refused** at admission;
its scorer later failed on file access.

Network-specific causality is **not isolated**: the real bridge instance also has
`Config.Env=["HOME=/tmp","TMPDIR=/tmp","PATH=/control-scorer"]`, whereas the
exact required order is PATH/HOME/TMPDIR. The strict list equality at :402 rejects
before the network clause at :404. These same three assignments have identical
meaning but Docker returned a different order. No observation was normalized or
rerun to conceal it. The image mismatch is checked first at :399; the extra-bind
probe has the required environment order and reaches the mount refusal. Focused
synthetic tests cover the network check, but this live probe alone does not.

Main stopped result **exists** at `evidence/inspect-03.json`. Removal receipt
**exists** at `evidence/docker-04.json`: returncode=0, stderr="", stdout is the
runtime name plus newline; native `evidence/verdict.json` cleanup has `removed=true`.
`evidence/post-removal-inspect.json`: returncode=1, stdout=`[]\n`, stderr=`error: no such
object: hg-pristine-476fa4a67f8f422e9f18e2d0027b32ba\n`.
`evidence/post-return-projection.json`: `/tmp/hg-pristine-sfa9udgm/input`, exists=false.
This is bounded object/projection cleanup evidence, not exhaustive namespace
absence. External work roots, negative-probe exports, image archive and historical
receipts are preserved; no snapshot was created. Snapshot lifecycle is not inferred.

## Diagnostic actor and exact invocation

`reports/w-chess3-control/actor.py` and `harness.json` are explicitly marked diagnostic control, not a candidate method. Before selecting or executing the released task, the actor's move rule was fixed to the lexicographically first legal UCI move from each current public FEN. It uses python-chess legality only, with no model, engine, search or puzzle-specific choice. The one connection stays open through newline-delimited observations/submissions; sends depend on `status=awaiting_move`, including turn zero, and stop on `solved`/`failed`. Actor-written events corroborate reception but never supply the score.

`tests/quality/test_chess3_diagnostic_actor.py` executes this unchanged actor in the real sandbox against fabricated multi-turn success and terminal-loss cases, checking turn-zero submission, one retained connection, legal moves, equality with controller-delivered observations, terminal reception and clean completion. These are synthetic protocol tests, separate from the single released-task run.

Exact command, cwd `/tmp/hg-chess3`, recorded in `reports/w-chess3-evidence/invocation.txt` and `execution/launch/command.txt`:

```sh
env HG_AGENT_BACKEND=mock python3 reports/w-chess3-evidence/launch.py --sampling diagnostic --rounds 0 --trials 1 --jobs 1 --authorize-unobserved-diagnostic 'Operator explicitly authorizes one fresh gated interactive ChessBench diagnostic using a deterministic protocol-correct reference actor marked as a diagnostic control, not a candidate method. Choose the lexicographically first legal UCI move from the current public FEN; no model calls, engine, search, gold access or puzzle tuning. One task, one trial, jobs=1, rounds=0. Existing gates, relay limits and unknown shared enforcement/lifecycle claims remain unchanged. Obtain a delivered terminal exchange and clean actor completion; raw controller evidence only, no comparable score publication, strict-replay comparison, expansion or production authorization.'

It reserves a new external receipt directory without resume/overwrite and stages only the control harness into the external work root. CSV pin and sandbox preflight are checked before actor launch. The actor has no dataset/reference access. Only the trusted controller receives the selected private verifier record.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Actor | `2000000000` | `4294967296` | `1024` | `true` |
| Verifier | `2000000000` | `4294967296` | `1024` | `true` |
| Service | `0` | `0` | `null` | `false` |

Actor/verifier each have `/tmp` tmpfs `rw,exec,nosuid,size=512m,mode=1777`. Service has no tmpfs entry. Thus actor/verifier have Docker-configured 2 CPU / 4 GiB / 1024 PID caps; the service has **no explicit per-container CPU/memory/PID cap**. This gap is retained; `eval/service/services.py:380-406` builds the service command without those resource flags. No limits were added or gates weakened for this run.

`cgroup-live-observations.json` records host reads of `/proc/<Pid>/cgroup` and the corresponding `/sys/fs/cgroup/...` files while verifier and service were live:

Exact cgroup source paths, raw `cpu.stat`, and observation times are in that file. The service's finite `pids.max=308691` is an observed daemon-provided cgroup value, **not** an explicit 1024 cap; its origin was not separately traced. The actor had already exited when these host cgroup reads occurred; its resource evidence is running Docker configuration, not a sampled actor cgroup file. Memory/PID current values are point samples, not peaks. No filesystem quota enforcement, disk maximum, dynamic dependency completeness or infallible resource enforcement is established.

`policy-input.json.context.limits` retains the requested resource/time/budget declaration: `cpus=2`, `memory_mb=4096`, `pids=1024`, actor/verifier deadlines 900 s, `max_steps=32`, `max_output_tokens_per_call=2048`; the service mismatch is explicit above. `execution/diagnostic_execution.json.identity_declared.config` confirms the unchanged actor defaults. ## Blocker decisions and validation

**AppWorld mounted-service control exists at one-task scope.** This removes the specific absence of an AppWorld observation in shared assessment S2/S5. It does not satisfy S2/S5 across other runtime classes/datasets or the complete action in `scorer-offline-closure`; S1-S6's remaining dataset/closure/valid-live-control requirements remain. Shared `scorer-offline-closure` stays `code-solvable`. `artifact-only-state-export` is unchanged.

**AppWorld successful-task-evidence remains open.** Its status remains `measurement`; raw `success=false` and staged `score=null` establish no authentic successful measured actor-task receipt. The registry appends this bounded failure to the retained historical evidence, with no successful-task closure or pilot authorization.

`reports/w-appworld-validation.txt` retains checker and focused pytest command/output. Initial focused validation: **82 passed, 1 failed**. The failure is the unchanged test stub at `tests/quality/test_appworld_service_diagnosis.py:79`, `lambda *a: None`, which rejects `run_id` keyword arguments passed by unchanged `eval/run/runner.py:160-162`. It is a stale test double, not the diagnostic result and not a reason to weaken admission. Both files match baseline tracked content. The original failing output is retained in `focused-tests-initial.txt`; no production or test patch was made around it. Final source-only registry tests after registry edits: **54 passed**. No additional benchmark diagnostic/control was launched for testing.

Final packet audit: all 64 inventoried evidence files are SHA-256 matched and staged, with the packet manifest also staged. `git diff --cached --check` reports whitespace only in byte-preserved pytest output and the service recording `environment_io.md`; these raw outputs were deliberately not normalized.

"""Explicit operator permission to attempt execution, never enforcement evidence.

This capability is platform-side only. It is not read from task metadata, model
output, environment variables or cache entries.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass

REASON = "policy_enforcement_not_observed"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Authorization:
    run_id: str
    task_id: str
    operator_authorization: str

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not all(isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() for v in (
                self.run_id, self.task_id, self.operator_authorization)):
            raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic requires run, task and explicit operator authorization")

    def require_scope(self, run_id, task_ids, *, trials=1, jobs=1, round_no=0):
        if (run_id != self.run_id or task_ids != [self.task_id]
                or type(trials) is not int or trials != 1
                or type(jobs) is not int or jobs != 1
                or type(round_no) is not int or round_no != 0):
            raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic authorization binds one run/task/trial/job at round 0")

    def record(self):
        return dict(stage="diagnostic", run_id=self.run_id, task_id=self.task_id,
                    operator_authorization=self.operator_authorization,
                    policy_enforcement_observed=False, measured=False,
                    production_authorized=False, comparable=False,
                    non_comparable_reason=REASON)


def validate_record(record, *, run_id, selected, stage="diagnostic"):
    if not isinstance(record, dict):
        raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic record must be an object")
    auth = Authorization(record.get("run_id"), record.get("task_id"),
                         record.get("operator_authorization"))
    auth.require_scope(run_id, selected)
    if (stage != "diagnostic" or record != auth.record() or any(
            record.get(k) is not False for k in (
                "policy_enforcement_observed", "measured", "production_authorized", "comparable"))):
        raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic cannot claim measurement, comparison or authorization")
"""Staged selection and immutable receipts; no model, scorer or runtime calls.

Evidence is supplied by the trusted orchestrator. Structural validation cannot
authenticate a gate/review or prove a runtime cleaned up. Unknown facts remain
explicit and cannot pass the expansion gate.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
from pathlib import Path

from eval.run.metrics import diagnostic_task_set, pilot_task_set
from eval.admission import lifecycle_receipt
from eval.policy import unobserved_diagnostic

SCHEMA = "harnessgrad.diagnostic-receipt.v2"
HASH_NAMES = ("source", "split", "manifest", "model", "harness")
#: The local endpoints a staged receipt may bind. **One entry, deliberately.** This
#: listing is what a receipt's `endpoint` is checked against, so an entry here is a
#: claim that the endpoint exists: leaving `8001` in after its server was retired on
#: 2026-10-14 would let a receipt attest to a binding nothing could serve. `8002` is
#: the one that was kept because `eval/unix_model_relay.py` routes the agent's model
#: there, so it is the endpoint a containerised harness actually reaches. Adding a
#: second server again means adding it back here at the same time.
ENDPOINTS = ("http://127.0.0.1:8002/v1",)


def _require(condition: bool, message: str) -> None:
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)


def _text(value) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip())


def _sha(value) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", value) is not None


def receipt_digest(receipt: dict | list) -> str:
    """Bind a separate review to the exact receipt, including all failed attempts."""
    body = json.dumps(receipt, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)
    return hashlib.sha256(body.encode()).hexdigest()


def eligible_digest(eligible: list[str]) -> str:
    """Bind the entire eligible ID pool without exporting its task payloads."""
    _require(isinstance(eligible, list) and all(_text(t) for t in eligible),
             "eligible IDs must be strings")
    return receipt_digest(sorted(set(eligible)))


def validate_context(context: dict) -> None:
    """Required evidence fields, with null accepted for explicitly unknown facts."""
    _require(isinstance(context, dict), "context must be an object")
    _require(_text(context.get("dataset")) and context.get("side") in ("train", "eval"),
             "context must bind dataset and side")
    metric = context.get("metric")
    _require(isinstance(metric, dict) and _text(metric.get("name"))
             and "scorer_sha256" in metric
             and (metric["scorer_sha256"] is None or _sha(metric["scorer_sha256"])),
             "metric requires a name and scorer_sha256 (or null)")
    hashes = context.get("hashes")
    _require(isinstance(hashes, dict) and all(
        key in hashes and (hashes[key] is None or _sha(hashes[key])) for key in HASH_NAMES),
        "source/split/manifest/model/harness hashes must be SHA256 or null")
    _require("endpoint" in context and (context["endpoint"] is None
             or context["endpoint"] in ENDPOINTS), "endpoint must bind one local GPU or be null")
    limits = context.get("limits")
    _require(isinstance(limits, dict) and all(
        key in limits and (limits[key] is None or (
            isinstance(limits[key], dict) and bool(limits[key]) and all(
                _text(k) and type(v) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(v) and v >= 0
                for k, v in limits[key].items()))) for key in ("resource", "budget")),
        "limits require resource and budget objects with nonnegative finite values (or null)")
    notes = context.get("comparability_notes")
    _require(isinstance(notes, list) and bool(notes) and all(_text(n) for n in notes),
             "comparability_notes must explicitly describe comparison limits")
    validate_lifecycle(context.get("gates"), "gates")


def validate_lifecycle(value: dict, name: str) -> None:
    _require(isinstance(value, dict) and value.get("status") in ("passed", "failed", "unknown")
             and "receipt_sha256" in value
             and (value["receipt_sha256"] is None or _sha(value["receipt_sha256"]))
             and _text(value.get("detail")), f"{name} requires status, receipt_sha256 and detail")
    _require(value["status"] != "passed" or _sha(value["receipt_sha256"]),
             f"{name} passed requires a receipt hash")


def staged_task_set(eligible: list[str], stage: str, *, trials: int = 1,
                    jobs: int = 1, rounds: int = 0) -> list[str]:
    _require(isinstance(eligible, list) and all(_text(t) for t in eligible),
             "eligible IDs must be strings")
    _require(stage in ("diagnostic", "pilot_10pct"), "unknown staged policy")
    _require(type(trials) is int and trials == 1 and type(jobs) is int and jobs == 1,
             "staged diagnostics and temporary pilots require trials=1, jobs=1")
    _require(type(rounds) is int and rounds == 0,
             "staged diagnostics and temporary pilots measure H0 only (rounds=0)")
    return diagnostic_task_set(eligible) if stage == "diagnostic" else pilot_task_set(eligible)


def measurement_summary(selected: list[str], outcomes: list[dict]) -> dict:
    """Partition one-trial outcomes. Missing records are unmeasured, never zero.

    Infrastructure/timeout/provider/scorer failures must be supplied as invalid
    or unmeasured. A measured zero requires a successful measurement contract.
    """
    _require(isinstance(selected, list) and all(_text(t) for t in selected)
             and len(set(selected)) == len(selected), "selected task IDs must be unique strings")
    _require(isinstance(outcomes, list), "outcomes must be a list")
    rows = {}
    for row in outcomes:
        _require(isinstance(row, dict) and row.get("task_id") in selected,
                 "outcome must name a selected task")
        tid = row["task_id"]
        _require(tid not in rows and type(row.get("trial")) is int and row["trial"] == 1,
                 "duplicate outcome or trial outside the one-trial plan")
        status = row.get("status")
        _require(status in ("measured", "invalid", "unmeasured"), "unknown outcome status")
        _require("score" in row and "reason" in row, "outcome requires explicit score and reason")
        if status == "measured":
            score = row["score"]
            _require(type(score) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(score)
                     and row["reason"] is None,
                     "measured outcome needs a finite score; metric-specific range is scorer-owned")
        else:
            _require(row["score"] is None and _text(row["reason"]),
                     "invalid/unmeasured outcomes require score=null and a reason")
        rows[tid] = copy.deepcopy(row)
    ordered = [rows.get(t, dict(task_id=t, trial=1, status="unmeasured", score=None,
                               reason="no outcome recorded")) for t in selected]
    measured = [r["score"] for r in ordered if r["status"] == "measured"]
    counts = {s: sum(r["status"] == s for r in ordered)
              for s in ("measured", "invalid", "unmeasured")}
    return {"outcomes": ordered, "score": sum(measured) / len(measured) if measured else None,
            "coverage": {"selected_tasks": len(selected), "expected_trials": len(selected),
                         **{f"{s}_{unit}": n for s, n in counts.items()
                            for unit in ("tasks", "trials")}}}


def scope_digest(*, stage: str, eligible_sha256: str, selected: list[str], context: dict) -> str:
    """Bind selection, provenance, metric, endpoint and limits before execution."""
    return receipt_digest(dict(stage=stage, eligible_task_ids_sha256=eligible_sha256,
                               selected_task_ids=selected, context=context))


def _scope_binding(receipt: dict) -> dict:
    return dict(run_id=receipt["run_id"], manifest_sha256=receipt["context"]["hashes"]["manifest"],
                scope_sha256=scope_digest(stage=receipt["stage"],
                                         eligible_sha256=receipt["eligible_task_ids_sha256"],
                                         selected=receipt["selected_task_ids"], context=receipt["context"]))


def _require_publication(receipt: dict, summary: dict) -> None:
    _require(receipt["context"]["gates"]["status"] == "passed" and all(
        receipt[k]["status"] == "passed" for k in ("cleanup", "snapshot")),
        "gates, cleanup and snapshot must pass before measured score publication")
    lifecycle_receipt.require_publication(
        receipt.get("lifecycle"), **_scope_binding(receipt),
        outcomes_sha256=receipt_digest(summary["outcomes"]),
        cleanup_sha256=receipt["cleanup"]["receipt_sha256"],
        snapshot_sha256=receipt["snapshot"]["receipt_sha256"])


def build_receipt(*, run_id: str, stage: str, eligible: list[str], selected: list[str],
                  context: dict, outcomes: list[dict], cleanup: dict, snapshot: dict,
                  lifecycle: dict | None = None, enforcement: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    """Create a receipt from observed evidence, including incomplete runs."""
    _require(_text(run_id), "run_id is required")
    _require(selected == staged_task_set(eligible, stage),
             "selected tasks must match the deterministic plan before execution")
    validate_context(context)
    validate_lifecycle(cleanup, "cleanup")
    validate_lifecycle(snapshot, "snapshot")
    if enforcement is not None:
        unobserved_diagnostic.validate_record(enforcement, run_id=run_id, selected=selected, stage=stage)
        # Execution permission is not a measurement contract, even if a scorer ran.
        outcomes = [dict(row, status="unmeasured", score=None,
                         reason=unobserved_diagnostic.REASON)
                    if row.get("status") == "measured" else row for row in outcomes]
    summary = measurement_summary(selected, outcomes)
    summary["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] = len(set(eligible))
    if lifecycle is not None:
        lifecycle_receipt.validate_lifecycle_receipt(lifecycle)
    receipt = dict(schema=SCHEMA, run_id=run_id, stage=stage, trials=1, jobs=1,
                   eligible_task_ids_sha256=eligible_digest(eligible),
                   selected_task_ids=list(selected), context=copy.deepcopy(context),
                   cleanup=copy.deepcopy(cleanup), snapshot=copy.deepcopy(snapshot),
                   lifecycle=copy.deepcopy(lifecycle), **summary)
    if enforcement is not None:
        receipt["enforcement"] = copy.deepcopy(enforcement)
    if lifecycle is not None:
        lifecycle_receipt.require_scope(lifecycle, **_scope_binding(receipt))
    if summary["coverage"]["measured_trials"]:
        if (lifecycle is None or not lifecycle["publication_ready"]
                or context["gates"]["status"] != "passed"
                or cleanup["status"] != "passed" or snapshot["status"] != "passed"):
            # Scores are provisional until lifecycle completion. Persist uncertainty,
            # never a measured zero, including individual task/trial score fields.
            withheld = [dict(row, status="unmeasured", score=None,
                             reason="score withheld: cleanup and snapshot lifecycle publication gate pending")
                        if row["status"] == "measured" else row for row in summary["outcomes"]]
            receipt.update(measurement_summary(selected, withheld))
            receipt["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] = len(set(eligible))
        else:
            _require_publication(receipt, summary)
    return receipt


def validate_receipt(receipt: dict) -> None:
    _require(isinstance(receipt, dict) and receipt.get("schema") == SCHEMA, "unsupported receipt schema")
    _require(_text(receipt.get("run_id")), "receipt run_id is required")
    _require(_sha(receipt.get("eligible_task_ids_sha256")), "eligible ID pool hash is required")
    staged_task_set([], receipt.get("stage"), trials=receipt.get("trials"), jobs=receipt.get("jobs"))
    validate_context(receipt.get("context"))
    if "enforcement" in receipt:
        unobserved_diagnostic.validate_record(
            receipt["enforcement"], run_id=receipt["run_id"],
            selected=receipt.get("selected_task_ids"), stage=receipt.get("stage"))
        _require(receipt.get("score") is None and not any(
            r.get("status") == "measured" for r in receipt.get("outcomes", [])),
            "unobserved diagnostic cannot publish measured scores")
    for name in ("cleanup", "snapshot"):
        validate_lifecycle(receipt.get(name), name)
    _require("lifecycle" in receipt, "receipt requires explicit lifecycle or null")
    if receipt["lifecycle"] is not None:
        lifecycle_receipt.validate_lifecycle_receipt(receipt["lifecycle"])
    summary = measurement_summary(receipt.get("selected_task_ids"), receipt.get("outcomes"))
    if receipt["lifecycle"] is not None:
        lifecycle_receipt.require_scope(receipt["lifecycle"], **_scope_binding(receipt))
    _require(summary["outcomes"] == receipt["outcomes"], "receipt must include every selected outcome")
    coverage = receipt.get("coverage")
    _require(isinstance(coverage, dict), "receipt coverage must be an object")
    eligible = coverage.get("eligible_tasks")
    _require(type(eligible) is int and eligible > 0, "receipt eligible_tasks must be positive")
    expected = 1 if receipt["stage"] == "diagnostic" else min(30, (eligible + 9) // 10)
    _require(len(receipt["selected_task_ids"]) == expected, "selected count differs from staged policy")
    _require(all(type(coverage.get(k)) is int and coverage[k] == v
                 for k, v in summary["coverage"].items()), "inconsistent task/trial coverage")
    _require("score" in receipt and receipt["score"] == summary["score"]
             and (receipt.get("score") is None or type(receipt["score"]) in (int, float)),
             "receipt score differs from measured outcomes")
    if summary["coverage"]["measured_trials"]:
        _require_publication(receipt, summary)


def evaluation_outcomes(result: dict, selected: list[str]) -> list[dict]:
    """Adapt a one-trial runner result without assigning zeros to failures."""
    _require(type(result.get("trials")) is int and result["trials"] == 1,
             "staged receipt requires one-trial evaluation evidence")
    rows = []
    for tid in selected:
        verdict = (result.get("verdicts") or {}).get(tid) or {}
        failure = (result.get("invalid") or {}).get(tid)
        if any((result.get("tampered") or {}).values()):
            status, score, reason = "invalid", None, "platform changed during evaluation"
        elif failure is not None:
            status, score, reason = "invalid", None, json.dumps(failure, sort_keys=True)
        elif verdict.get("unmeasured"):
            status, score, reason = "unmeasured", None, str(verdict["unmeasured"])
        elif tid in (result.get("not_run") or []) or tid in (result.get("harness_failed") or {}):
            status, score, reason = "invalid", None, "harness did not run or reported a runtime failure"
        elif tid in (result.get("cached_tasks") or []):
            status, score, reason = "unmeasured", None, "cached result; no fresh diagnostic trial"
        elif tid in (result.get("harness_output") or {}) and (
                type(result["harness_output"][tid].get("exit_code")) is not int
                or result["harness_output"][tid]["exit_code"] != 0):
            # run_one records timeouts as exit -1. A trace with usage can keep
            # harness_failed empty, and the general runner can retain an artifact
            # score after a crash. Neither proves completed staged execution.
            exit_code = result["harness_output"][tid].get("exit_code")
            status = "invalid" if type(exit_code) is int else "unmeasured"
            score, reason = None, f"staged execution did not complete with integer exit 0: {exit_code!r}"
        elif tid in (result.get("per_task") or {}):
            status, score, reason = "measured", result["per_task"][tid], None
        else:
            status, score, reason = "unmeasured", None, "no outcome recorded"
        rows.append(dict(task_id=tid, trial=1, status=status, score=score, reason=reason))
    return rows


def require_review(bundle: dict, *, context: dict, eligible: list[str], run_id: str | None = None) -> None:
    """Expansion requires a distinct, hash-bound review of a complete diagnostic."""
    _require(isinstance(bundle, dict), "diagnostic review must be an object")
    receipt = bundle.get("receipt")
    validate_receipt(receipt)
    _require("enforcement" not in receipt,
             "unobserved diagnostic cannot authorize expansion")
    _require(receipt["stage"] == "diagnostic", "expansion requires a diagnostic receipt")
    _require(receipt["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] == len(set(eligible))
             and receipt["eligible_task_ids_sha256"] == eligible_digest(eligible)
             and receipt["selected_task_ids"] == diagnostic_task_set(eligible),
             "diagnostic does not match the current eligible pool")
    validate_context(context)
    for key in ("dataset", "side", "metric", "hashes", "endpoint", "limits", "gates"):
        _require(receipt["context"][key] == context[key], f"diagnostic context changed: {key}")
    _require(all(_sha(context["hashes"][k]) for k in HASH_NAMES)
             and _sha(context["metric"]["scorer_sha256"]) and context["endpoint"] in ENDPOINTS
             and all(context["limits"][k] is not None for k in ("resource", "budget")),
             "unknown provenance, endpoint or limits cannot authorize expansion")
    _require(context["gates"]["status"] == "passed" and all(
        receipt[k]["status"] == "passed" for k in ("cleanup", "snapshot")),
        "gates, cleanup and snapshot must have passing evidence")
    _require(receipt["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == 1
             and receipt["coverage"]["invalid_trials"] == 0
             and receipt["coverage"]["unmeasured_trials"] == 0,
             "expansion requires a valid measured diagnostic")
    review = bundle.get("review")
    _require(isinstance(review, dict) and review.get("approved") is True
             and _text(review.get("reviewer")) and _text(review.get("reviewed_at"))
             and review.get("receipt_sha256") == receipt_digest(receipt),
             "separate approved review must bind the exact diagnostic receipt")
    _require(run_id is None or run_id != receipt["run_id"], "expansion requires a fresh run ID")


def write_receipt(path: Path, receipt: dict) -> None:
    """Exclusive creation preserves old and incomplete evidence."""
    validate_receipt(receipt)
    body = json.dumps(receipt, indent=2, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False) + "\n"
    with Path(path).open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        stream.write(body)


def reserve_run_directory(root: Path, run_id: str) -> Path:
    """A fresh staged ID is a single directory component, never a resume target."""
    _require(_text(run_id) and run_id not in (".", "..")
             and Path(run_id).name == run_id, "run_id must be a single directory name")
    path = Path(root) / run_id
    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    return path

$ cat eval/admission/runner_admission.py eval/admission/lifecycle_receipt.py eval/policy/runtime_policy.py
"""Offline admission contracts for trusted adapter runtime observations.

These functions inspect supplied facts only. They install no quotas, execute no
probes and grant no production authorization. A backend must independently collect
fresh observations and apply the declared bounds. Snapshot and cleanup checks are
best-effort: missing facts retain uncertainty, not an infallibility proof gate
(operator ruling, 2026-10-15; docs/enforcement-proof-decision.md).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from functools import wraps
import hashlib
import json
import posixpath
import re

MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES = 16 * 1024
MAX_SURFACES = 32
MAX_OWNED = 64
RESOURCE_KEYS = {"cpu_millis", "memory_bytes", "swap_bytes", "pids", "timeout_ms"}
KINDS = {"container", "network", "snapshot", "volume", "workspace"}


class AdmissionError(RuntimeError):
    """A refused contract, with a bounded, unmeasured receipt for persistence."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, receipt: dict | None = None):
        super().__init__(code)
        self.code = code
        self.receipt = receipt


def _require(ok: bool, code: str) -> None:
    if not ok:
        raise AdmissionError(code)


def _object(value, keys: set[str], code: str) -> None:
    _require(isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == keys, code)


def _integer(value, code: str, *, positive: bool = False) -> None:
    _require(type(value) is int and (1 if positive else 0) <= value <= 2**63 - 1, code)


def _text(value, code: str) -> None:
    _require(isinstance(value, str) and 0 < len(value) <= 256, code)
    try:
        _require(len(value.encode()) <= 256 and not any(ord(c) < 32 for c in value), code)
    except UnicodeError as exc:
        raise AdmissionError(code) from exc


def _sha(value, code: str) -> None:
    _require(isinstance(value, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[a-f0-9]{64}", value) is not None, code)


def _hash(value) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"),
                                    allow_nan=False).encode()).hexdigest()


def _binding(value) -> dict:
    _object(value, {"adapter_id", "run_id", "trial_id", "manifest_sha256"}, "invalid_binding")
    for key in ("adapter_id", "run_id", "trial_id"):
        _text(value[key], "invalid_binding")
    _sha(value["manifest_sha256"], "invalid_binding")
    return dict(value)


def _gate(stage):
    def decorate(check):
        @wraps(check)
        def wrapped(binding, policy, observed):
            receipt = dict(schema="harnessgrad.runner-admission.v1", stage=stage,
                           status="blocked", measured=False, production_authorized=False)
            if stage == "cleanup":
                receipt["lease_action"] = "quarantine"
            try:
                receipt["binding"] = _binding(binding)
                evidence = check(binding, policy, observed)
                receipt.update(status="passed", policy_sha256=_hash(policy),
                               observation_sha256=_hash(observed), evidence=evidence)
                if stage == "cleanup":
                    receipt["lease_action"] = "release"
                _require(len(json.dumps(receipt).encode()) <= MAX_RECEIPT_BYTES,
                         "receipt_too_large")
                return receipt
            except AdmissionError as exc:
                receipt.pop("evidence", None)
                receipt.update(status="blocked", reason=exc.code)
                if stage == "cleanup":
                    receipt["lease_action"] = "quarantine"
                raise AdmissionError(exc.code, receipt) from exc
        return wrapped
    return decorate


def _observation(binding, observed, keys):
    _object(observed, keys | {"binding", "evidence_sha256"}, "invalid_observation")
    _require(_binding(observed["binding"]) == binding, "stale_observation")
    _sha(observed["evidence_sha256"], "invalid_evidence_hash")


def _partial_object(value, keys, code):
    _require(isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) <= keys, code)


def _fact(observed, key, uncertainties, prefix=""):
    """Omitted/null lifecycle facts are unknown; present facts still get checked."""
    value = observed.get(key)
    if value is None:
        uncertainties.append(prefix + key)
    return value


def _optional_hash(observed, uncertainties, prefix=""):
    value = _fact(observed, "evidence_sha256", uncertainties, prefix)
    if value is not None:
        _sha(value, "invalid_evidence_hash")


def _lifecycle_observation(binding, observed, keys, uncertainties):
    # Identity and schema integrity remain mandatory. Only lifecycle observation
    # coverage is best-effort; stale facts and unchecked payloads are not accepted.
    _partial_object(observed, keys | {"binding", "evidence_sha256"}, "invalid_observation")
    _require("binding" in observed, "invalid_observation")
    _require(_binding(observed["binding"]) == binding, "stale_observation")
    _optional_hash(observed, uncertainties)


def _resources(values, code):
    _object(values, RESOURCE_KEYS, code)
    for key, value in values.items():
        _integer(value, code, positive=key != "swap_bytes")


def _surfaces(value):
    _require(isinstance(value, dict) and len(value) <= MAX_SURFACES, "invalid_surfaces")
    for path in value:
        _text(path, "invalid_surface_path")
        _require(path.startswith("/") and path != "/" and not path.startswith("//")
                 and posixpath.normpath(path) == path, "invalid_surface_path")


@_gate("runtime")
def admit_runtime(binding: dict, policy: dict, observed: dict) -> dict:
    """Check actual resource/network limits and every writable filesystem surface."""
    _object(policy, {"resources", "network", "filesystems"}, "invalid_runtime_policy")
    _resources(policy["resources"], "invalid_resource_policy")
    _require(policy["network"] == "none", "network_policy_not_supported")
    _surfaces(policy["filesystems"])
    for limits in policy["filesystems"].values():
        _object(limits, {"bytes", "inodes"}, "invalid_filesystem_policy")
        for value in limits.values():
            _integer(value, "invalid_filesystem_policy", positive=True)
    _observation(binding, observed, {"resources", "resource_backend", "timeout_backend", "network",
                                   "attachments", "read_only_root", "filesystems"})
    _resources(observed["resources"], "invalid_resource_observation")
    _require(observed["resource_backend"] in ("cgroup-v1", "cgroup-v2"),
             "resource_enforcement_unproven")
    _require(observed["timeout_backend"] == "host-supervisor", "deadline_enforcement_unproven")
    _require(observed["resources"] == policy["resources"], "resource_limit_mismatch")
    _require(observed["network"] == "none" and observed["attachments"] == [],
             "network_enforcement_unproven")
    _require(observed["read_only_root"] is True, "writable_root_unbounded")
    _surfaces(observed["filesystems"])
    _require(set(observed["filesystems"]) == set(policy["filesystems"]),
             "writable_surface_inventory_mismatch")
    scopes = set()
    for path, limits in policy["filesystems"].items():
        actual = observed["filesystems"][path]
        _object(actual, {"bytes", "inodes", "backend", "scope_id", "evidence_sha256"},
                "filesystem_enforcement_unproven")
        _require(actual["backend"] in ("tmpfs", "project-quota"),
                 "filesystem_enforcement_unproven")
        _text(actual["scope_id"], "invalid_quota_scope")
        _require(actual["scope_id"] not in scopes, "shared_quota_scope")
        scopes.add(actual["scope_id"])
        _sha(actual["evidence_sha256"], "invalid_evidence_hash")
        for key in ("bytes", "inodes"):
            _integer(actual[key], "invalid_filesystem_observation", positive=True)
            _require(actual[key] == limits[key], "filesystem_limit_mismatch")
    return dict(resources=dict(observed["resources"]), network="none", read_only_root=True,
                filesystems={path: dict(row) for path, row in observed["filesystems"].items()})


@_gate("snapshot")
def admit_snapshot(binding: dict, policy: dict, observed: dict) -> dict:
    """Check available quiescence, headroom and deadline facts best-effort.

    This is preflight capacity evidence, not writable-layer quota enforcement.
    The snapshot worker's CPU/memory/PID limits need a separate runtime admission.
    Missing/null facts are retained as uncertainty; known failures still refuse.
    Admission does not attest snapshot completion or prove bounded peak usage.
    """
    _object(policy, {"max_bytes", "max_inodes", "reserve_bytes", "reserve_inodes", "timeout_ms"},
            "invalid_snapshot_policy")
    for value in policy.values():
        _integer(value, "invalid_snapshot_policy", positive=True)
    uncertainties = []
    keys = {"writable_bytes", "writable_inodes", "free_bytes", "free_inodes",
            "running", "exit_code", "oom_killed", "timeout_ms", "device_id"}
    _lifecycle_observation(binding, observed, keys, uncertainties)
    facts = {key: _fact(observed, key, uncertainties) for key in sorted(keys)}
    for key in ("running", "oom_killed"):
        if facts[key] is not None:
            _require(facts[key] is False, "snapshot_quiescence_unproven")
    if facts["exit_code"] is not None:
        _require(type(facts["exit_code"]) is int and facts["exit_code"] == 0,
                 "snapshot_quiescence_unproven")
    if facts["device_id"] is not None:
        _text(facts["device_id"], "invalid_snapshot_device")
    for key in ("writable_bytes", "writable_inodes", "free_bytes", "free_inodes"):
        if facts[key] is not None:
            _integer(facts[key], "invalid_snapshot_observation")
    if facts["timeout_ms"] is not None:
        _integer(facts["timeout_ms"], "invalid_snapshot_deadline", positive=True)
        _require(facts["timeout_ms"] == policy["timeout_ms"], "snapshot_deadline_mismatch")
    for unit in ("bytes", "inodes"):
        writable, free = facts["writable_" + unit], facts["free_" + unit]
        if writable is not None:
            _require(writable <= policy["max_" + unit], "snapshot_size_exceeded")
        # Even without a size observation, free space below the reserve is bad.
        if free is not None:
            _require(free >= (writable or 0) + policy["reserve_" + unit],
                     "snapshot_headroom_insufficient")
        facts["required_" + unit] = (None if writable is None
                                     else writable + policy["reserve_" + unit])
    return dict(facts, limits=dict(policy), best_effort=True,
                uncertainties=sorted(uncertainties))


def _inventory(value):
    _require(isinstance(value, list) and len(value) <= MAX_OWNED, "invalid_owned_inventory")
    identities = set()
    for item in value:
        _object(item, {"kind", "id"}, "invalid_owned_inventory")
        _require(isinstance(item["kind"], str) and item["kind"] in KINDS, "invalid_owned_kind")
        _text(item["id"], "invalid_owned_id")
        identity = (item["kind"], item["id"])
        _require(identity not in identities, "duplicate_owned_resource")
        identities.add(identity)
    return identities


@_gate("cleanup")
def admit_cleanup(binding: dict, policy: dict, observed: dict) -> dict:
    """Check available owned-object/namespace facts, retaining coverage gaps.

    Missing absence evidence no longer quarantines a lease by itself. Known
    remaining resources, wrong identities and failures still refuse release.
    This check neither performs cleanup nor proves an atomic ledger update.
    """
    _object(policy, {"owned", "timeout_ms"}, "invalid_cleanup_policy")
    owned = _inventory(policy["owned"])
    _integer(policy["timeout_ms"], "invalid_cleanup_policy", positive=True)
    uncertainties = []
    _lifecycle_observation(binding, observed, {"objects", "namespaces", "timeout_ms"},
                           uncertainties)
    timeout = _fact(observed, "timeout_ms", uncertainties)
    if timeout is not None:
        _require(timeout == policy["timeout_ms"] and type(timeout) is int,
                 "cleanup_deadline_mismatch")
    objects = _fact(observed, "objects", uncertainties)
    if objects is None:
        objects = []
    _require(isinstance(objects, list) and len(objects) <= MAX_OWNED, "invalid_cleanup_objects")
    identities = []
    for index, item in enumerate(objects):
        _partial_object(item, {"kind", "id", "absent", "evidence_sha256"}, "invalid_cleanup_object")
        prefix = f"objects[{index}]."
        kind = _fact(item, "kind", uncertainties, prefix)
        resource_id = _fact(item, "id", uncertainties, prefix)
        if kind is not None:
            _require(isinstance(kind, str) and kind in KINDS, "invalid_owned_kind")
        if resource_id is not None:
            _text(resource_id, "invalid_owned_id")
        if kind is not None or resource_id is not None:
            _require(any((kind is None or kind == expected_kind)
                         and (resource_id is None or resource_id == expected_id)
                         for expected_kind, expected_id in owned), "cleanup_inventory_mismatch")
        if kind is not None and resource_id is not None:
            identities.append(dict(kind=kind, id=resource_id))
        absent = _fact(item, "absent", uncertainties, prefix)
        if absent is not None:
            _require(absent is True, "owned_absence_unproven")
        _optional_hash(item, uncertainties, prefix)
    checked = _inventory(identities)
    _require(checked <= owned, "cleanup_inventory_mismatch")
    for index, item in enumerate(policy["owned"]):
        if (item["kind"], item["id"]) not in checked:
            uncertainties.append(f"owned[{index}]")
    namespaces = _fact(observed, "namespaces", uncertainties)
    if namespaces is None:
        namespaces = {}
    _partial_object(namespaces, KINDS, "cleanup_namespace_coverage_incomplete")
    checked_kinds = []
    for kind in sorted(KINDS):
        row = _fact(namespaces, kind, uncertainties, "namespaces.")
        if row is None:
            continue
        _partial_object(row, {"remaining", "evidence_sha256"}, "invalid_cleanup_namespace")
        prefix = f"namespaces.{kind}."
        remaining = _fact(row, "remaining", uncertainties, prefix)
        if remaining is not None:
            _require(remaining == [], "owned_namespace_absence_unproven")
            checked_kinds.append(kind)
        _optional_hash(row, uncertainties, prefix)
    return dict(owned_count=len(owned), observed_owned_count=len(checked),
                checked_kinds=checked_kinds, timeout_ms=timeout,
                limits=dict(timeout_ms=policy["timeout_ms"]), best_effort=True,
                uncertainties=sorted(uncertainties))
"""Pure, immutable lifecycle receipts for trusted orchestration.

No execution, observation collection, deletion, lease updates or authorization.
Digests bind supplied evidence; they do not authenticate its origin. Backends
must collect facts independently of the actor and persist each returned receipt.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json

from eval.admission import runner_admission as admission

SCHEMA = "harnessgrad.lifecycle-receipt.v1"
ATTESTATION_SCHEMA = "harnessgrad.lifecycle-attestation.v1"
STAGES = ("scope", "admit", "execute", "quiesce", "score", "seal", "cleanup")
MAX_BYTES = 128 * 1024


def digest(value) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"),
                                    allow_nan=False).encode()).hexdigest()


def _require(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)


def _bounded(value):
    _require(len(json.dumps(value, allow_nan=False).encode()) <= MAX_BYTES,
             "lifecycle receipt too large")


def start_lifecycle(binding: dict, scope_sha256: str) -> dict:
    """Freeze scope before admission; scope hash names the pre-execution plan."""
    admission._binding(binding)
    admission._sha(scope_sha256, "invalid_scope_hash")
    return dict(schema=SCHEMA, binding=copy.deepcopy(binding), scope_sha256=scope_sha256,
                state="scope", status="passed", events=[], publication_ready=False,
                production_authorized=False)


def _gate(binding, stage, evidence):
    # Recheck original supplied facts with the shared gate, rather than trusting a
    # free-standing status boolean or an adapter-provided admission receipt.
    if isinstance(evidence, dict) and set(evidence) == {"refusal"}:
        # A persisted refusal can only deny progress. Never persist rejected raw
        # input, which may include task payloads, private logs or credentials.
        receipt = evidence["refusal"]
        keys = {"schema", "stage", "status", "measured", "production_authorized", "binding", "reason"}
        if stage == "cleanup":
            keys.add("lease_action")
        _require(isinstance(receipt, dict) and keys <= set(receipt)
                 and set(receipt) <= keys | {"policy_sha256", "observation_sha256"}
                 and receipt["schema"] == "harnessgrad.runner-admission.v1"
                 and receipt["stage"] == stage and receipt["status"] == "blocked"
                 and receipt["measured"] is False and receipt["production_authorized"] is False
                 and admission._binding(receipt["binding"]) == binding
                 and (stage != "cleanup" or receipt["lease_action"] == "quarantine"),
                 "invalid persisted admission refusal")
        admission._text(receipt["reason"], "invalid_refusal_reason")
        for key in ("policy_sha256", "observation_sha256"):
            if key in receipt:
                admission._sha(receipt[key], "invalid_refusal_hash")
        return receipt
    _require(isinstance(evidence, dict) and set(evidence) == {"policy", "observed"},
             "admission evidence requires policy and observed facts")
    try:
        return getattr(admission, "admit_" + stage)(binding, **evidence)
    except admission.AdmissionError as exc:
        _require(exc.receipt is not None, "missing admission refusal receipt")
        return exc.receipt


def _attestation(binding, stage, evidence):
    _require(isinstance(evidence, dict) and set(evidence) == {
        "schema", "binding", "stage", "status", "receipt_sha256", "facts", "detail"},
        "invalid lifecycle attestation")
    _require(evidence["schema"] == ATTESTATION_SCHEMA and evidence["stage"] == stage,
             "wrong lifecycle attestation stage")
    _require(admission._binding(evidence["binding"]) == binding, "stale lifecycle attestation")
    _require(evidence["status"] in ("passed", "failed", "unknown"), "invalid attestation status")
    admission._text(evidence["detail"], "invalid_attestation_detail")
    _require(isinstance(evidence["facts"], dict), "invalid attestation facts")
    if evidence["status"] != "passed":
        _require(evidence["facts"] == {}, "failed/unknown attestation must not claim passing facts")
        if evidence["receipt_sha256"] is not None:
            admission._sha(evidence["receipt_sha256"], "invalid_attestation_hash")
        return evidence
    admission._sha(evidence["receipt_sha256"], "invalid_attestation_hash")
    facts = evidence["facts"]
    if stage in ("execute", "quiesce"):
        keys = {"exit_code", "oom_killed", "timed_out"}
        if stage == "quiesce":
            keys.add("running")
        _require(set(facts) == keys and type(facts["exit_code"]) is int
                 and facts["exit_code"] == 0 and facts["oom_killed"] is False
                 and facts["timed_out"] is False
                 and (stage != "quiesce" or facts["running"] is False),
                 "successful execution/quiescence unproven")
    elif stage == "score":
        _require(set(facts) == {"outcomes_sha256"}, "invalid scoring attestation")
        admission._sha(facts["outcomes_sha256"], "invalid_outcomes_hash")
    elif stage == "seal":
        _require(set(facts) == {"outcomes_sha256", "snapshot"}, "invalid seal attestation")
        admission._sha(facts["outcomes_sha256"], "invalid_outcomes_hash")
        snapshot = facts["snapshot"]
        _attestation(binding, "snapshot", snapshot)
        _require(snapshot["status"] == "passed", "snapshot completion must pass before seal")
    elif stage == "snapshot":
        # A preflight receipt alone cannot attest completed snapshot/export work.
        _require(set(facts) == {"completed", "artifact_sha256", "exit_code", "oom_killed",
                               "timed_out", "preflight", "runtime"}, "invalid snapshot completion attestation")
        _require(facts["completed"] is True and type(facts["exit_code"]) is int
                 and facts["exit_code"] == 0 and facts["oom_killed"] is False
                 and facts["timed_out"] is False, "snapshot completion unproven")
        admission._sha(facts["artifact_sha256"], "invalid_snapshot_artifact_hash")
        _require(_gate(binding, "snapshot", facts["preflight"])["status"] == "passed",
                 "snapshot preflight must pass")
        _require(_gate(binding, "runtime", facts["runtime"])["status"] == "passed",
                 "snapshot worker runtime admission must pass")
    return evidence


def _advance(receipt, stage, evidence):
    _require(stage in STAGES[1:], "unknown lifecycle stage")
    _require(receipt["state"] != "cleanup", "cleanup is terminal; use a fresh run ID")
    if receipt["status"] != "passed":
        _require(stage == "cleanup", "failed lifecycle may only attempt cleanup")
    else:
        _require(stage == STAGES[STAGES.index(receipt["state"]) + 1],
                 "lifecycle stage skipped or repeated")
    if stage in ("admit", "cleanup"):
        checked = _gate(receipt["binding"], "runtime" if stage == "admit" else stage, evidence)
        status = "passed" if checked["status"] == "passed" else "failed"
        if status == "failed":
            evidence = {"refusal": checked}
    else:
        checked = _attestation(receipt["binding"], stage, evidence)
        status = checked["status"]
    if stage == "seal" and status == "passed":
        scored = receipt["events"][-1]["evidence"]["facts"]["outcomes_sha256"]
        _require(checked["facts"]["outcomes_sha256"] == scored, "sealed outcomes differ from scoring")
    result = copy.deepcopy(receipt)
    result["events"].append(dict(stage=stage, status=status,
                                 evidence=copy.deepcopy(evidence), receipt_sha256=digest(checked)))
    result["state"] = stage
    # Failed/unknown work remains terminal for scoring even after successful cleanup.
    result["status"] = status if receipt["status"] == "passed" else receipt["status"]
    result["publication_ready"] = stage == "cleanup" and result["status"] == "passed"
    _bounded(result)
    return result


def validate_lifecycle_receipt(receipt: dict) -> None:
    """Replay checks without trusting derived states, statuses or event digests."""
    _require(isinstance(receipt, dict) and set(receipt) == {
        "schema", "binding", "scope_sha256", "state", "status", "events",
        "publication_ready", "production_authorized"} and receipt["schema"] == SCHEMA,
        "unsupported lifecycle receipt")
    _bounded(receipt)
    _require(isinstance(receipt["events"], list) and len(receipt["events"]) <= 6,
             "invalid lifecycle event count")
    replay = start_lifecycle(receipt["binding"], receipt["scope_sha256"])
    for event in receipt["events"]:
        _require(isinstance(event, dict) and set(event) == {
            "stage", "status", "evidence", "receipt_sha256"}, "invalid lifecycle event")
        replay = _advance(replay, event["stage"], event["evidence"])
    _require(replay == receipt and type(receipt["publication_ready"]) is bool
             and receipt["production_authorized"] is False, "inconsistent lifecycle receipt")


def transition(receipt: dict, stage: str, evidence: dict) -> dict:
    """Return a new checkpoint. No skipped/repeated phases or post-failure scoring."""
    validate_lifecycle_receipt(receipt)
    return _advance(receipt, stage, evidence)


def require_scope(receipt: dict, *, run_id: str, manifest_sha256: str, scope_sha256: str) -> None:
    """Check even incomplete checkpoints against their frozen publication scope."""
    validate_lifecycle_receipt(receipt)
    _require(receipt["binding"]["run_id"] == run_id
             and receipt["binding"]["manifest_sha256"] == manifest_sha256
             and receipt["scope_sha256"] == scope_sha256, "publication scope/binding changed")


def require_publication(receipt: dict, *, run_id: str, manifest_sha256: str,
                        scope_sha256: str, outcomes_sha256: str,
                        cleanup_sha256: str, snapshot_sha256: str) -> None:
    """Permit structural publication only for this exact scope and sealed result."""
    require_scope(receipt, run_id=run_id, manifest_sha256=manifest_sha256, scope_sha256=scope_sha256)
    _require(receipt["publication_ready"], "cleanup and snapshot lifecycle must pass before publication")
    seal, cleanup = receipt["events"][-2:]
    facts = seal["evidence"]["facts"]
    _require(facts["outcomes_sha256"] == outcomes_sha256, "publication outcomes changed after seal")
    _require(cleanup["receipt_sha256"] == cleanup_sha256
             and digest(facts["snapshot"]) == snapshot_sha256,
             "publication cleanup and snapshot attestations differ")
"""Trusted phase limits and private agent package projections."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path

from eval.observe.tracing import _materialize


def admit_native_network(env: dict, manifest: dict, agent_backend: str) -> None:
    """Refuse a declared offline task before launching a network-dependent agent."""
    network = env.get("network")
    if network not in (None, "none"):
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported native sandbox network policy: {network!r}")
    if network == "none" and manifest.get("role") != "control" \
            and agent_backend != "mock":
        raise ValueError(
            "network='none' cannot run a live agent backend; use an offline control "
            "or the mock backend")


def phase_timeout(*limits, default: float = 3600) -> float:
    """Honor every explicit bound; an omitted caller limit is only a fallback."""
    values = []
    for limit in limits:
        if limit is None:
            continue
        if isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, (int, float)) \
                or not math.isfinite(limit) or limit <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid phase timeout: {limit!r}")
        values.append(limit)
    return min(values) if values else default


def stage_agent_runtime(spec: dict, root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Build an allowlisted package outside agent-writable task storage."""
    package = Path(spec["package_dir"])
    if not package.is_absolute() or not package.is_dir() or package.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError("agent_runtime.package_dir must be an existing absolute package directory")
    files = spec.get("files")
    if not isinstance(files, list) or not files:
        raise ValueError("agent_runtime.files must be a nonempty allowlist")
    if root.exists() and any(root.iterdir()):
        raise ValueError("agent_runtime projection directory must be empty")
    names = set()
    for entry in files:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict) or not isinstance(entry.get("path"), str):
            raise ValueError("agent_runtime files need explicit relative paths")
        path = Path(entry["path"])
        if not path.parts or path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts \
                or "__pycache__" in path.parts or path.suffix in (".pyc", ".pyo"):
            raise ValueError("agent_runtime file path is unsafe or contains bytecode")
        if path in names:
            raise ValueError("agent_runtime file paths must be unique")
        names.add(path)
        if ("content" in entry) == ("from" in entry):
            raise ValueError("agent_runtime files need exactly one content or from source")
        if "from" in entry:
            source = Path(entry["from"])
            if not source.is_absolute() or source.is_symlink() or not source.is_file():
                raise ValueError("agent_runtime sources must be absolute regular files")
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _materialize(root, files, Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2])
    if not (root / "__init__.py").is_file():
        raise ValueError("agent_runtime requires __init__.py")
    return root, package

## Decision, scope limits and validation

Registry change: **none**. Both original shared objects/statuses/next_actions,
all 23 references to each (46 total), all dataset/historical records and the pinned
assertions in `tests/quality/test_dataset_blockers.py` stay byte-for-byte unchanged.
No clause is deleted, downgraded or reworded to manufacture a closure. Partial
progress and the satisfied S2 clause are recorded here without removing the
blocker's remaining requirements. No production code, adapter, frozen receipt or
historical evidence is modified. Commit is confined to this isolated worktree.

`docs/enforcement-proof-decision.md` remains governing: snapshot/cleanup are
best-effort with uncertainty retained; known failures/binding mismatches still
refuse. This reassessment does not restore withdrawn filesystem-quota proof,
atomic leases, exhaustive absence, peak bounds or infallible enforcement as new
gates. The remaining clauses concern missing dataset contracts, available
dependencies, actual provenance/measurement scope and applicable live validation.

It would be false to keep saying pristine/AppWorld have no real controls, GAIA/Tau
defaults remain movable, or only nine official/local components were executed.
It would also be false to turn class exercise into dataset breadth, fifteen bounded
components into all-dataset coverage, fixed access/measurement/evidence-retention
defects into ownership/quiescence/equivalence validation, or AppWorld's new service
flags into historical live caps. The invalid original pristine attempt remains
invalid; the fixed final control is genuinely measured; the four staged dataset
diagnostics remain unmeasured publications.

Validation commands and exact checker/test output are recorded in
`reports/w-reassess-validation.txt`: checker **valid=true**, 28 datasets, two
shared blockers, 11 historical records, production_authorized=false; **54 focused
registry tests passed** with pytest basetemp outside the platform tree. These
source-only checks validate registry structure, verbatim evidence locators and
preserved history, not runtime readiness.

     1	"""Execute a harness on a task set. Framework-owned (INTERFACE.md §0)."""
     2	from __future__ import annotations
