$ sed -n 1,220p tests/quality/test_unobserved_diagnostic_c2.py
"""Synthetic execution at the runtime boundary; no dataset, model or Docker calls."""
import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import jsonschema
import pytest

from eval.policy import diagnostic_policy as policy
from eval.policy.unobserved_diagnostic import Authorization, REASON
from eval.run import runner
from test_staged_receipt_audit_codex193 import context, stubbed_driver, SELECTED

TASK = {"task_id": SELECTED[0], "goal": "synthetic diagnostic"}
EXEC = {"kind": "exec", "state": "container", "network": "none", "image": "synthetic:no-execution"}
AUTH = Authorization("synthetic-driver-193", TASK["task_id"], "Operator authorizes synthetic diagnostic")
SCHEMA = json.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "eval/diagnostic_receipt.schema.json").read_text())


@pytest.fixture
def synthetic_runtime(monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    def execute(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append((args, kwargs))
        return dict(task_id=TASK["task_id"], trace='{"synthetic":true}\n',
                    exit_code=0, stderr="", verdict=dict(passed=True, score=0.25))
    monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "_run_one", execute)
    monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "snapshot", lambda *a: {})
    return calls


def test_unauthorized_exec_refuses_before_launch_and_cache(tmp_path, synthetic_runtime):
    result = runner.run_one(tmp_path, TASK, env=EXEC)
    assert result["invalid"]["receipt"]["reason"] == "backend_observation_unavailable"
    cache = {f"sha:{TASK['task_id']}:1": {"score": 1}}
    result = runner.evaluate(tmp_path, [TASK], {}, cache, harness_sha="sha",
                             envs={TASK["task_id"]: EXEC})
    assert result["invalid"][TASK["task_id"]]["receipt"]["measured"] is False
    assert not synthetic_runtime


def test_authorized_exec_runs_and_tags_every_result_without_cache_publication(tmp_path, synthetic_runtime):
    cache = {}
    direct = runner.run_one(tmp_path, TASK, env=EXEC, run_id=AUTH.run_id,
                            diagnostic_authorization=AUTH)
    assert direct["enforcement"] == AUTH.record()
    result = runner.evaluate(tmp_path, [TASK], {}, cache, envs={TASK["task_id"]: EXEC},
                             run_id=AUTH.run_id, diagnostic_authorization=AUTH,
                             progress_dir=tmp_path / "records")
    assert len(synthetic_runtime) == 2
    assert result["enforcement"] == AUTH.record()
    assert result["per_task"] == {TASK["task_id"]: 0.25}  # raw scorer fact only
    assert result["scorer_isolation_class"] == "unknown"
    assert result["scorer_isolation_comparable"] is False
    assert cache == {}
    progress = json.loads((tmp_path / "records/progress/round-0" / f"{TASK['task_id']}.json").read_text())
    assert progress["enforcement"] == AUTH.record()
    assert progress["scorer_isolation_class"] == "unknown"
    assert progress["scorer_isolation_comparable"] is False
    assert progress["score"] == 0.25 and progress["complete"] is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", [dict(run_id="other"), dict(trials=2), dict(jobs=2),
                                    dict(round_no=1), dict(tasks=[TASK, TASK]),
                                    dict(cache={"old": {"score": 1}})])
def test_authorization_cannot_expand_scope(tmp_path, synthetic_runtime, change):
    args = dict(repo=tmp_path, tasks=[TASK], scorable={}, envs={TASK["task_id"]: EXEC},
                run_id=AUTH.run_id, diagnostic_authorization=AUTH)
    args.update(change)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        runner.evaluate(**args)
    assert not synthetic_runtime


@pytest.fixture
def diagnostic_driver(monkeypatch, stubbed_driver):
    driver, run_dir, workspace = stubbed_driver
    import sys
    evidence = Path(sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--policy-evidence") + 1])
    content = json.loads(evidence.read_text())
    content["context"]["gates"] = dict(status="unknown", receipt_sha256=None,
                                      detail="Policy enforcement not observed")
    evidence.write_text(json.dumps(content))
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver.datasets, "load_envs", lambda *a: {TASK["task_id"]: EXEC})
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver.container, "available", lambda: "synthetic")
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver.container, "self_check", lambda *a: dict(ok=True, detail="synthetic"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver.platform_config, "current", lambda: SimpleNamespace(
        agent=SimpleNamespace(base_url=policy.ENDPOINTS[0], backend="openai")))
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver.platform_config, "dump", lambda **k: {})
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver, "evaluate", runner.evaluate)
    from harnessgrad.records import _write_run_meta
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver, "_write_run_meta", _write_run_meta)
    monkeypatch.setattr(driver, "_write_curve", lambda *a: pytest.fail("No diagnostic curve allowed"))
    return driver, run_dir


def test_unknown_gates_without_operator_permission_still_refuse(diagnostic_driver, synthetic_runtime):
    driver, run_dir = diagnostic_driver
    assert driver.main() == 2
    assert not synthetic_runtime and not run_dir.exists()


def test_operator_authorized_driver_runs_once_but_cannot_publish_or_expand(
        monkeypatch, diagnostic_driver, synthetic_runtime):
    import sys
    driver, run_dir = diagnostic_driver
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", sys.argv + ["--authorize-unobserved-diagnostic", AUTH.operator_authorization])
    assert driver.main() == 2  # execution may finish, comparable publication must refuse
    assert len(synthetic_runtime) == 1
    progress = json.loads((run_dir / "progress/round-0" / f"{TASK['task_id']}.json").read_text())
    assert progress["enforcement"] == AUTH.record() and progress["complete"] is True
    assert progress["scorer_isolation_class"] == "unknown"
    assert progress["scorer_isolation_comparable"] is False
    receipt = json.loads((run_dir / "diagnostic_receipt.json").read_text())
    policy.validate_receipt(receipt)
    jsonschema.validate(receipt, SCHEMA)
    assert receipt["score"] is None and receipt["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == 0
    assert receipt["coverage"]["unmeasured_trials"] == 1
    assert receipt["outcomes"][0]["reason"] == REASON
    for name in ("staged_plan.json", "diagnostic_receipt.json", "run_meta.json", "diagnostic_execution.json"):
        assert json.loads((run_dir / name).read_text())["enforcement"] == AUTH.record()
    assert not (run_dir / "curve.jsonl").exists()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="cannot authorize expansion"):
        policy.require_review(dict(receipt=receipt), context=receipt["context"], eligible=SELECTED)
    forged = copy.deepcopy(receipt)
    forged["enforcement"]["measured"] = True
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        policy.validate_receipt(forged)
    with pytest.raises(jsonschema.ValidationError):
        jsonschema.validate(forged, SCHEMA)
    before = (run_dir / "diagnostic_receipt.json").read_bytes()
    assert driver.main() == 2
    assert len(synthetic_runtime) == 1 and (run_dir / "diagnostic_receipt.json").read_bytes() == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", ["preflight", "exception"])
def test_incomplete_authorized_attempt_has_final_nonobservation_receipt(
        monkeypatch, diagnostic_driver, synthetic_runtime, failure):
    import sys
    driver, run_dir = diagnostic_driver
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", sys.argv + ["--authorize-unobserved-diagnostic", AUTH.operator_authorization])
    if failure == "preflight":
        monkeypatch.setattr(driver, "_resolve_envs", lambda envs: (envs, "missing synthetic image"))
        assert driver.main() == 2
        assert not synthetic_runtime
    else:
        def fail(*a, **k):
            raise FileNotFoundError("missing synthetic runtime file")
        monkeypatch.setattr(runner, "_run_one", fail)
        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError, match="missing synthetic runtime"):
            driver.main()
    receipt = json.loads((run_dir / "diagnostic_receipt.json").read_text())
    policy.validate_receipt(receipt)
    assert receipt["enforcement"] == AUTH.record()
    assert receipt["coverage"]["invalid_trials"] == 1 and receipt["score"] is None
    assert not (run_dir / "curve.jsonl").exists()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("extra", [["--sampling", "all"], ["--sampling", "pilot_10pct"],
                                   ["--rounds", "1"], ["--trials", "2"], ["--jobs", "2"],
                                   ["--side", "eval"]])
def test_operator_flag_never_authorizes_other_driver_paths(monkeypatch, diagnostic_driver, synthetic_runtime, extra):
    import sys
    driver, run_dir = diagnostic_driver
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", sys.argv + ["--authorize-unobserved-diagnostic", AUTH.operator_authorization] + extra)
    assert driver.main() == 2
    assert not synthetic_runtime and not run_dir.exists()

# Real production pristine diagnostic attempt - w-pristine

**The production pristine path executed, but the answer check did not.** A real
stopped Docker instance passed admission and both projection rechecks, started,
then exited on `Permission denied` reading the exported answer. The native verdict
returned `score=0.0`; this is an **invalid/unmeasured scorer failure**, not a genuine
zero. No production fix, gate relaxation, UID override or second production attempt
was made. Unexpected image/network/mount controls genuinely refused before start.

Artifact paths below are relative to `tests/fixtures/hg_wpristine/`; paths starting
with `eval/`, `docs/` or `reports/` are repository-relative. Every raw captured original remains under
`/tmp/hg-wpristine-runtime/w-pristine-control-20261014T225600Z-7cbd8f16/evidence/`
as well. The audited receipt and later summaries are additional files in the fixture,
not replacements for original receipts. `SHA256SUMS` pins the complete fixture.

## Real failure, audited counts and production defects

`evidence/docker-02.json` records `docker start --attach` returncode=1, stdout="",
and exact stderr:

```text
/control-scorer/check.sh: line 5: can't open /control-input/answer.txt: Permission denied
```

No `completed_check=fail` or `completed_check=pass` marker was emitted. The scorer
did read its private expected dependency first, but failed before comparing answers.
`evidence/inspect-03.json`: `Status="exited"`, `Running=false`, `ExitCode=1`,
`OOMKilled=false`, `Error=""`, StartedAt=`2026-10-14T22:51:55.278462757Z`,
FinishedAt=`2026-10-14T22:51:55.431904266Z`. Normal process exit is not evidence
that the scoring predicate completed.

1. **Unreadable platform export:** `eval/admission/artifact_admission.py:280,289,292`
   creates the projection directories mode 0700 and files 0600, owned by the calling
   user. Observed exported file ownership/mode is UID 1026/0600. The created image
   has `Config.User=""` (default container root), and
   `eval/verification/pristine.py:67-82` drops all capabilities without aligning
   the container user to export ownership. `evidence/inspect-01.json` corroborates those
   actual settings. On this host it prevents reading the projection. Retained
   negative-probe export directories also actually stat as UID 1026/mode 0700
   (`evidence/host-observations.json`). No chmod/chown/user/capability workaround was used.
2. **Invalid scorer exit becomes numeric zero:**
   `eval/verification/pristine.py:100-108` checks Docker state/OOM/error and maps
   every normal nonzero exit to score 0. This shell initialization failure passes
   that condition. For the generic exit-status protocol, scorer initialization
   and a completed failing check are indistinguishable; this authored control's
   completion marker exposes the difference. No production code was changed.
3. **Stopped result not retained natively:** pristine.py:97-103 reads stopped
   State but does not put it into `scorer_isolation_evidence`; :115-118 retains
   only cleanup name/removal Boolean. Raw inspection/removal evidence exists here
   because the external observer saved the real outputs, not because the native
   verdict contains a stopped-result field.

`evidence/measurement-audit.json`, derived by the recorded audit command from
unchanged raw artifacts, is the measurement decision:

| Control coverage / measurement | Value |
| --- | --- |
| eligible / selected tasks / expected trials | `1 / 1 / 1` |
| completed answer checks / genuine measured zeros | `0 / 0` |
| measured / unmeasured / invalid trials | `0 / 0 / 1` (disjoint counts; invalid means no valid measurement) |
| publishable score | `null` |
| raw native verdict score | `0.0` (invalid for this metric) |
| formal lifecycle / production_authorized | `null / false` |

These disjoint counts follow `eval/policy/diagnostic_policy.py:108-114`: scorer
failures must be invalid or unmeasured; a measured zero requires a successful
measurement contract. The initial `evidence/audited-control-receipt.json` treated invalid
as a subset of unmeasured (0/1/1); it is preserved as a superseded provisional
classification. The final `evidence/measurement-audit.json` partitions the single trial.

The executed driver's first count inference used the numeric verdict and therefore
wrote `measured_trials=1, unmeasured_trials=0, invalid_trials=0` to the original
`evidence/control-receipt.json`. That artifact and `evidence/driver-executed.py` remain unchanged and
are explicitly superseded by the audit, never republished as valid measurement.
inspect returncode=1. Each commands file contains the real create, inspect, remove
and absent-object inspection outputs. Refusal source is :399-412 (image/network)
and :426-436 (binds). The compliant main instance was **not refused** at admission;
its scorer later failed on file access.

Network-specific causality is **not isolated**: the real bridge instance also has
`Config.Env=["HOME=/tmp","TMPDIR=/tmp","PATH=/control-scorer"]`, whereas the
exact required order is PATH/HOME/TMPDIR. The strict list equality at :402 rejects
before the network clause at :404. These same three assignments have identical
meaning but Docker returned a different order. No observation was normalized or
rerun to conceal it. The image mismatch is checked first at :399; the extra-bind
probe has the required environment order and reaches the mount refusal. Focused
synthetic tests cover the network check, but this live probe alone does not.

Main stopped result **exists** at `evidence/inspect-03.json`. Removal receipt
**exists** at `evidence/docker-04.json`: returncode=0, stderr="", stdout is the
runtime name plus newline; native `evidence/verdict.json` cleanup has `removed=true`.
`evidence/post-removal-inspect.json`: returncode=1, stdout=`[]\n`, stderr=`error: no such
object: hg-pristine-476fa4a67f8f422e9f18e2d0027b32ba\n`.
`evidence/post-return-projection.json`: `/tmp/hg-pristine-sfa9udgm/input`, exists=false.
This is bounded object/projection cleanup evidence, not exhaustive namespace

$ sed -n 1,260p tests/quality/test_diagnostic_policy.py
"""Pure policy/evidence controls. No model, H0, containers or official scorers."""
import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from eval.policy import diagnostic_policy as policy
from lifecycle_fixtures_codex177 import completed_lifecycle, summaries

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
ELIGIBLE = [f"task-{i}" for i in range(492)]


def attestation(status="passed"):
    return dict(status=status, receipt_sha256="a" * 64 if status == "passed" else None,
                detail="synthetic unit-test evidence; no runtime authorization")


@pytest.fixture
def context():
    return dict(dataset="demo", side="train",
                metric=dict(name="unit-test metric", scorer_sha256="b" * 64),
                hashes={k: "c" * 64 for k in policy.HASH_NAMES},
                endpoint=policy.ENDPOINTS[0], gates=attestation(),
                limits=dict(resource=dict(filesystem_bytes=1024),
                            budget=dict(model_calls=1, wall_seconds=1)),
                comparability_notes=["Synthetic fixture; not evidence for a production pilot."])


def receipt(context, status="measured", score=0.0):
    selected = policy.staged_task_set(ELIGIBLE, "diagnostic")
    outcomes = [dict(task_id=selected[0], trial=1, status=status,
                     score=score if status == "measured" else None,
                     reason=None if status == "measured" else "scorer unavailable")]
    lifecycle = (completed_lifecycle(context, ELIGIBLE, selected, outcomes)
                 if context["hashes"]["manifest"] is not None else None)
    cleanup, snapshot = summaries(lifecycle) if lifecycle else (attestation(), attestation())
    return policy.build_receipt(
        run_id="unit-diagnostic", stage="diagnostic", eligible=ELIGIBLE, selected=selected,
        context=context, cleanup=cleanup, snapshot=snapshot, outcomes=outcomes, lifecycle=lifecycle)


def reviewed(row):
    return dict(receipt=row, review=dict(approved=True, reviewer="unit-test reviewer",
                                        reviewed_at="2026-10-14T00:00:00Z",
                                        receipt_sha256=policy.receipt_digest(row)))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stage,count", [("diagnostic", 1), ("pilot_10pct", 30)])
def test_staged_size_and_no_scores(stage, count):
    assert len(policy.staged_task_set(ELIGIBLE, stage)) == count
    assert policy.staged_task_set(ELIGIBLE, stage) == policy.staged_task_set(ELIGIBLE[::-1], stage)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("config", [dict(trials=3), dict(jobs=2), dict(rounds=1),
                                   dict(trials=True), dict(jobs=1.0), dict(rounds=False)])
def test_historical_or_parallel_protocol_is_refused(config):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        policy.staged_task_set(ELIGIBLE, "pilot_10pct", **config)


def test_measured_zero_can_pass_separate_review(context):
    row = receipt(context)
    policy.validate_receipt(row)
    policy.require_review(reviewed(row), context=context, eligible=ELIGIBLE, run_id="fresh-unit-pilot")
    assert row["score"] == 0.0 and row["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == 1

def test_failure_is_null_and_never_authorizes_expansion(context, status):

    policy.validate_receipt(row)

def test_missing_attempts_are_explicit_unmeasured(context):
    selected = policy.staged_task_set(ELIGIBLE, "pilot_10pct")
    row = policy.build_receipt(run_id="unit-incomplete", stage="pilot_10pct", eligible=ELIGIBLE,
                               selected=selected, context=context, outcomes=[],
                               cleanup=attestation("unknown"), snapshot=attestation("unknown"))
    policy.validate_receipt(row)
    assert row["coverage"]["unmeasured_trials"] == 30
    assert row["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] == 492 and row["score"] is None
    assert all(r["score"] is None and r["reason"] for r in row["outcomes"])


def test_mixed_outcomes_average_only_measured_zeros_and_scores():
    selected = ["zero", "one", "invalid", "missing"]
    rows = [dict(task_id=t, trial=1, status=s, score=v, reason=r) for t, s, v, r in (
        ("zero", "measured", 0.0, None), ("one", "measured", 1.0, None),
        ("invalid", "invalid", None, "provider failed"))]
    summary = policy.measurement_summary(selected, rows)
    assert summary["score"] == 0.5
    assert summary["coverage"]["measured_tasks"] == 2
    assert summary["coverage"]["invalid_tasks"] == 1
    assert summary["coverage"]["unmeasured_tasks"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("score", [float("nan"), float("inf"), True, None])
def test_nonfinite_or_non_numeric_measured_scores_are_refused(score):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        policy.measurement_summary(["t"], [dict(task_id="t", trial=1, status="measured",
                                              score=score, reason=None)])


def test_metric_scale_is_not_assumed_to_be_unit_interval():
    summary = policy.measurement_summary(
        ["scaled"], [dict(task_id="scaled", trial=1, status="measured",
                          score=65.6, reason=None)])
    assert summary["score"] == 65.6

def test_stale_or_changed_receipt_and_pool_are_refused(context):
    bundle = reviewed(receipt(context))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fresh run ID"):
        policy.require_review(bundle, context=context, eligible=ELIGIBLE, run_id="unit-diagnostic")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="eligible pool"):
        policy.require_review(bundle, context=context, eligible=ELIGIBLE[:-1])
    changed_pool = list(ELIGIBLE)
    changed_index = next(i for i, t in enumerate(changed_pool)
                         if t != bundle["receipt"]["selected_task_ids"][0])
    changed_pool[changed_index] = "replacement-task"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="eligible pool"):
        policy.require_review(bundle, context=context, eligible=changed_pool)
    bundle["receipt"]["context"]["comparability_notes"].append("changed after review")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="scope/binding changed"):
        policy.require_review(bundle, context=context, eligible=ELIGIBLE)


def test_schema_matches_complete_and_incomplete_receipts(context):
    jsonschema = pytest.importorskip("jsonschema")
    schema = json.loads((ROOT / "eval/diagnostic_receipt.schema.json").read_text())
    jsonschema.Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)
    for status in ("measured", "invalid", "unmeasured"):
        row = receipt(context, status)
        jsonschema.validate(row, schema)
        policy.validate_receipt(row)
    bad = receipt(context, "invalid")
    bad["outcomes"][0]["score"] = 0.0
    with pytest.raises(jsonschema.ValidationError):
        jsonschema.validate(bad, schema)


def test_receipt_writes_and_run_reservations_preserve_old_evidence(context, tmp_path):
    root = policy.reserve_run_directory(tmp_path, "unit-fresh")
    path = root / "receipt.json"
    policy.write_receipt(path, receipt(context, "unmeasured"))
    before = path.read_bytes()
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        policy.write_receipt(path, receipt(context))
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        policy.reserve_run_directory(tmp_path, "unit-fresh")
    assert path.read_bytes() == before

"""Synthetic lifecycle/regression checks; forbid external execution and sockets."""
from copy import deepcopy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess

import pytest

from eval.policy import diagnostic_policy as policy
from eval.admission import lifecycle_receipt as lifecycle
from lifecycle_fixtures_codex177 import attest, completed_lifecycle, stage_evidence, summaries

BINDING = dict(adapter_id="synthetic-adapter-177", run_id="unit-diagnostic",
               trial_id="synthetic-trial-1", manifest_sha256="c" * 64)
OUTCOMES = [dict(task_id="synthetic-task-177", trial=1, status="measured", score=0.0, reason=None)]


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def no_external_execution(monkeypatch):
    def refuse(*args, **kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("synthetic lifecycle attempted external execution")
    monkeypatch.setattr(subprocess, "run", refuse)
    monkeypatch.setattr(subprocess, "Popen", refuse)
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket, "socket", refuse)


@pytest.fixture
def context():
    return dict(dataset="synthetic-dataset-177", side="train",
                metric=dict(name="synthetic metric", scorer_sha256="b" * 64),
                hashes={key: "c" * 64 for key in policy.HASH_NAMES},
                endpoint=policy.ENDPOINTS[0], limits=dict(resource=dict(bytes=1), budget=dict(calls=1)),
                gates=dict(status="passed", receipt_sha256="a" * 64, detail="synthetic gate"),
                comparability_notes=["Synthetic tests only; no model/H0 or production evidence."])


def checkpoint(stop="scope"):
    receipt = lifecycle.start_lifecycle(BINDING, "a" * 64)
    for stage, evidence in stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES).items():
        if receipt["state"] == stop:
            break
        receipt = lifecycle.transition(receipt, stage, evidence)
    return receipt


def test_all_checkpoints_are_immutable_and_only_cleanup_permits_publication():
    receipt = checkpoint()
    for stage, evidence in stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES).items():
        before = deepcopy((receipt, evidence))
        advanced = lifecycle.transition(receipt, stage, evidence)
        assert (receipt, evidence) == before
        assert advanced["publication_ready"] == (stage == "cleanup")
        assert advanced["production_authorized"] is False
        assert "score" not in advanced
        lifecycle.validate_lifecycle_receipt(advanced)
        receipt = advanced
    assert len(json.dumps(receipt).encode()) < lifecycle.MAX_BYTES


@pytest.mark.parametrize("current", lifecycle.STAGES)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("requested", lifecycle.STAGES[1:])
def test_skips_repeats_and_cleanup_before_seal_are_refused(current, requested):
    receipt = checkpoint(current)
    if current != "cleanup" and requested == lifecycle.STAGES[lifecycle.STAGES.index(current) + 1]:
        return
    before = deepcopy(receipt)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        lifecycle.transition(receipt, requested, stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES)[requested])
    assert receipt == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stage", ["execute", "quiesce", "score", "seal"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", ["failed", "unknown"])
def test_failures_allow_cleanup_but_never_measurement(stage, status):
    previous = lifecycle.STAGES[lifecycle.STAGES.index(stage) - 1]
    receipt = lifecycle.transition(checkpoint(previous), stage, attest(BINDING, stage, status=status))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="only attempt cleanup"):
        lifecycle.transition(receipt, "score", stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES)["score"])
    final = lifecycle.transition(receipt, "cleanup", stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES)["cleanup"])
    assert final["status"] == status and not final["publication_ready"]
    assert final["events"][-1]["status"] == "passed"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stage", ["admit", "cleanup"])
def test_admission_and_cleanup_refusals_are_preserved(stage):
    receipt = checkpoint("scope" if stage == "admit" else "seal")
    facts = stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES)[stage]
    if stage == "admit":
        facts["observed"]["read_only_root"] = False
    else:
        facts["observed"]["objects"][0]["absent"] = False
    final = lifecycle.transition(receipt, stage, facts)
    lifecycle.validate_lifecycle_receipt(final)
    assert final["status"] == "failed" and not final["publication_ready"]
    refusal = final["events"][-1]["evidence"]["refusal"]
    assert refusal["status"] == "blocked"
    if stage == "cleanup":
        assert refusal["lease_action"] == "quarantine"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("key", BINDING)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("stage", lifecycle.STAGES[1:])
def test_cross_run_trial_adapter_and_manifest_evidence_cannot_publish(stage, key):
    receipt = checkpoint(lifecycle.STAGES[lifecycle.STAGES.index(stage) - 1])
    facts = stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES)[stage]
    binding = facts["observed"]["binding"] if stage in ("admit", "cleanup") else facts["binding"]
    binding[key] = "f" * 64 if key == "manifest_sha256" else "stale-synthetic-identity"
    if stage in ("admit", "cleanup"):
        assert not lifecycle.transition(receipt, stage, facts)["publication_ready"]
    else:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="stale"):
            lifecycle.transition(receipt, stage, facts)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", ["preflight_only", "missing_artifact", "timeout", "oom", "exit",
                                   "inodes", "binding", "outcomes", "worker_runtime"])
def test_seal_requires_matching_result_and_completed_snapshot(change):
    evidence = stage_evidence(BINDING, OUTCOMES)["seal"]
    snapshot = evidence["facts"]["snapshot"]
    facts = snapshot["facts"]
    if change == "preflight_only":
        evidence["facts"]["snapshot"] = facts["preflight"]
    elif change == "missing_artifact":
        facts["artifact_sha256"] = None
    elif change == "timeout":
        facts["timed_out"] = True
    elif change == "oom":
        facts["oom_killed"] = True
    elif change == "exit":
        facts["exit_code"] = 137
    elif change == "inodes":
        facts["preflight"]["observed"]["free_inodes"] = 0
    elif change == "binding":
        snapshot["binding"]["run_id"] = "stale-synthetic-run"
    elif change == "outcomes":
        evidence["facts"]["outcomes_sha256"] = "f" * 64
    elif change == "worker_runtime":
        facts["runtime"]["observed"]["read_only_root"] = False
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, RuntimeError)):
        lifecycle.transition(checkpoint("score"), "seal", evidence)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mutation", [
    lambda r: r.update(publication_ready=True), lambda r: r.update(production_authorized=True),
    lambda r: r.update(state="cleanup"), lambda r: r["events"].pop(0),
    lambda r: r["events"][-1].update(status="failed"),
    lambda r: r["events"][-1].update(receipt_sha256="f" * 64),
])
def test_replay_refuses_tampered_state_status_and_event_receipts(mutation):
    receipt = checkpoint("seal")
    mutation(receipt)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        lifecycle.validate_lifecycle_receipt(receipt)


def diagnostic(context, *, complete=True, cleanup_status="passed", snapshot_status="passed"):
    selected = [OUTCOMES[0]["task_id"]]
    receipt = completed_lifecycle(context, selected, selected, OUTCOMES)
    cleanup, snapshot = summaries(receipt)
    if cleanup_status != "passed":
        cleanup.update(status=cleanup_status, receipt_sha256=None)
    if snapshot_status != "passed":
        snapshot.update(status=snapshot_status, receipt_sha256=None)
    return policy.build_receipt(run_id=BINDING["run_id"], stage="diagnostic", eligible=selected,
                                selected=selected, context=context, outcomes=OUTCOMES,
                                cleanup=cleanup, snapshot=snapshot,
                                lifecycle=receipt if complete else None)


def test_completed_lifecycle_publishes_valid_measured_zero(context):
    receipt = diagnostic(context)
    policy.validate_receipt(receipt)
    assert receipt["score"] == 0.0 and receipt["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("field", ["cleanup_status", "snapshot_status"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", ["failed", "unknown"])
def test_pending_attestations_withhold_all_scores_and_recompute_coverage(context, field, status):
    receipt = diagnostic(context, **{field: status})
    policy.validate_receipt(receipt)
    assert receipt["score"] is None and receipt["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == 0
    assert receipt["coverage"]["unmeasured_trials"] == 1
    assert receipt["outcomes"][0]["score"] is None and receipt["outcomes"][0]["reason"]
    assert OUTCOMES[0]["score"] == 0.0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stage", lifecycle.STAGES)
def test_each_pending_checkpoint_withholds_scores_until_cleanup(context, stage):
    selected = [OUTCOMES[0]["task_id"]]
    receipt = completed_lifecycle(context, selected, selected, OUTCOMES)
    cleanup, snapshot = summaries(receipt)
    checkpoint_receipt = lifecycle.start_lifecycle(receipt["binding"], receipt["scope_sha256"])
    for step, evidence in stage_evidence(receipt["binding"], OUTCOMES).items():
        if checkpoint_receipt["state"] == stage:
            break
        checkpoint_receipt = lifecycle.transition(checkpoint_receipt, step, evidence)
    row = policy.build_receipt(run_id=BINDING["run_id"], stage="diagnostic", eligible=selected,
                               selected=selected, context=context, outcomes=OUTCOMES,
                               cleanup=cleanup, snapshot=snapshot, lifecycle=checkpoint_receipt)
    policy.validate_receipt(row)
    assert row["score"] == (0.0 if stage == "cleanup" else None)
    assert row["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == (1 if stage == "cleanup" else 0)

    receipt["outcomes"] = deepcopy(OUTCOMES)

def require_publication(receipt: dict, *, run_id: str, manifest_sha256: str,
                        scope_sha256: str, outcomes_sha256: str,
                        cleanup_sha256: str, snapshot_sha256: str) -> None:
    """Permit structural publication only for this exact scope and sealed result."""
    require_scope(receipt, run_id=run_id, manifest_sha256=manifest_sha256, scope_sha256=scope_sha256)
    _require(receipt["publication_ready"], "cleanup and snapshot lifecycle must pass before publication")
    seal, cleanup = receipt["events"][-2:]
    facts = seal["evidence"]["facts"]
    _require(facts["outcomes_sha256"] == outcomes_sha256, "publication outcomes changed after seal")
    _require(cleanup["receipt_sha256"] == cleanup_sha256
             and digest(facts["snapshot"]) == snapshot_sha256,
             "publication cleanup and snapshot attestations differ")

64: - Invalid, unmeasured, timeout, scorer, provider, cleanup or snapshot failures are not ordinary zero scores.

406:                 raise ValueError("policy evidence must be an object")
407:             context = policy_evidence.get("context")
408:             diagnostic_policy.validate_context(context)
409:             if (context["dataset"] != args.dataset or context["side"] != args.side
410:                     or (context["gates"]["status"] != "passed" and not (
411:                         args.authorize_unobserved_diagnostic
412:                         and context["gates"]["status"] == "unknown"))):
413:                 raise ValueError("passing dataset gates must bind this dataset and side")
414:         except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
598:         return 2
599: 
600:     diagnostic_authorization = (Authorization(run_id, side_ids[0],
601:                                 args.authorize_unobserved_diagnostic)
602:                                 if args.authorize_unobserved_diagnostic else None)
603:     enforcement = diagnostic_authorization.record() if diagnostic_authorization else None
604: 
605:     def save_policy_receipt(res: dict | None = None) -> bool:
606:         if not staged:
607:             return True
608:         unknown = {"status": "unknown", "receipt_sha256": None,
609:                    "detail": "driver has no verified lifecycle receipt; trusted orchestration must attest"}
610:         receipt = diagnostic_policy.build_receipt(
611:             run_id=run_id, stage=args.sampling, eligible=eligible_ids, selected=side_ids,
612:             context=policy_evidence["context"],
613:             outcomes=(diagnostic_policy.evaluation_outcomes(res, side_ids) if res is not None else []),
614:             cleanup=unknown, snapshot=unknown, enforcement=enforcement)
615:         # Keep the pre-execution plan even if evaluation is interrupted or refused.
616:         diagnostic_policy.write_receipt(
617:             run_dir / ("diagnostic_receipt.json" if res is not None else "staged_plan.json"), receipt)
618:         return (enforcement is None and receipt["lifecycle"] is not None
619:                 and receipt["lifecycle"]["publication_ready"]
620:                 and receipt["coverage"]["measured_tasks"] == len(side_ids))

eval/policy/unobserved_diagnostic.py
17:     def __post_init__(self):
18:         if not all(isinstance(v, str) and v.strip() for v in (
19:                 self.run_id, self.task_id, self.operator_authorization)):
20:             raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic requires run, task and explicit operator authorization")
21: 
22:     def require_scope(self, run_id, task_ids, *, trials=1, jobs=1, round_no=0):
23:         if (run_id != self.run_id or task_ids != [self.task_id]
24:                 or type(trials) is not int or trials != 1
25:                 or type(jobs) is not int or jobs != 1
26:                 or type(round_no) is not int or round_no != 0):
27:             raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic authorization binds one run/task/trial/job at round 0")
28: 
29:     def record(self):
30:         return dict(stage="diagnostic", run_id=self.run_id, task_id=self.task_id,
31:                     operator_authorization=self.operator_authorization,
32:                     policy_enforcement_observed=False, measured=False,
33:                     production_authorized=False, comparable=False,
34:                     non_comparable_reason=REASON)

37: def validate_record(record, *, run_id, selected, stage="diagnostic"):
38:     if not isinstance(record, dict):
39:         raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic record must be an object")
40:     auth = Authorization(record.get("run_id"), record.get("task_id"),
41:                          record.get("operator_authorization"))
42:     auth.require_scope(run_id, selected)
43:     if (stage != "diagnostic" or record != auth.record() or any(
44:             record.get(k) is not False for k in (
45:                 "policy_enforcement_observed", "measured", "production_authorized", "comparable"))):
46:         raise ValueError("unobserved diagnostic cannot claim measurement, comparison or authorization")

eval/policy/diagnostic_policy.py
1: """Staged selection and immutable receipts; no model, scorer or runtime calls.
2: 
3: Evidence is supplied by the trusted orchestrator. Structural validation cannot
4: authenticate a gate/review or prove a runtime cleaned up. Unknown facts remain
5: explicit and cannot pass the expansion gate.
6: """
87: def validate_lifecycle(value: dict, name: str) -> None:
88:     _require(isinstance(value, dict) and value.get("status") in ("passed", "failed", "unknown")
89:              and "receipt_sha256" in value
90:              and (value["receipt_sha256"] is None or _sha(value["receipt_sha256"]))
91:              and _text(value.get("detail")), f"{name} requires status, receipt_sha256 and detail")
92:     _require(value["status"] != "passed" or _sha(value["receipt_sha256"]),
93:              f"{name} passed requires a receipt hash")
94: 
160: def _require_publication(receipt: dict, summary: dict) -> None:
161:     _require(receipt["context"]["gates"]["status"] == "passed" and all(
162:         receipt[k]["status"] == "passed" for k in ("cleanup", "snapshot")),
163:         "gates, cleanup and snapshot must pass before measured score publication")
164:     lifecycle_receipt.require_publication(
165:         receipt.get("lifecycle"), **_scope_binding(receipt),
166:         outcomes_sha256=receipt_digest(summary["outcomes"]),
167:         cleanup_sha256=receipt["cleanup"]["receipt_sha256"],
168:         snapshot_sha256=receipt["snapshot"]["receipt_sha256"])
169: 
170: 
171: def build_receipt(*, run_id: str, stage: str, eligible: list[str], selected: list[str],
172:                   context: dict, outcomes: list[dict], cleanup: dict, snapshot: dict,
173:                   lifecycle: dict | None = None, enforcement: dict | None = None) -> dict:
174:     """Create a receipt from observed evidence, including incomplete runs."""
175:     _require(_text(run_id), "run_id is required")
176:     _require(selected == staged_task_set(eligible, stage),
177:              "selected tasks must match the deterministic plan before execution")
178:     validate_context(context)
179:     validate_lifecycle(cleanup, "cleanup")
180:     validate_lifecycle(snapshot, "snapshot")
181:     if enforcement is not None:
182:         unobserved_diagnostic.validate_record(enforcement, run_id=run_id, selected=selected, stage=stage)
183:         # Execution permission is not a measurement contract, even if a scorer ran.
184:         outcomes = [dict(row, status="unmeasured", score=None,
185:                          reason=unobserved_diagnostic.REASON)
186:                     if row.get("status") == "measured" else row for row in outcomes]
187:     summary = measurement_summary(selected, outcomes)
188:     summary["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] = len(set(eligible))
189:     if lifecycle is not None:
190:         lifecycle_receipt.validate_lifecycle_receipt(lifecycle)
191:     receipt = dict(schema=SCHEMA, run_id=run_id, stage=stage, trials=1, jobs=1,
192:                    eligible_task_ids_sha256=eligible_digest(eligible),
193:                    selected_task_ids=list(selected), context=copy.deepcopy(context),
194:                    cleanup=copy.deepcopy(cleanup), snapshot=copy.deepcopy(snapshot),
195:                    lifecycle=copy.deepcopy(lifecycle), **summary)
196:     if enforcement is not None:
197:         receipt["enforcement"] = copy.deepcopy(enforcement)
198:     if lifecycle is not None:
199:         lifecycle_receipt.require_scope(lifecycle, **_scope_binding(receipt))
200:     if summary["coverage"]["measured_trials"]:
201:         if (lifecycle is None or not lifecycle["publication_ready"]
202:                 or context["gates"]["status"] != "passed"
203:                 or cleanup["status"] != "passed" or snapshot["status"] != "passed"):
204:             # Scores are provisional until lifecycle completion. Persist uncertainty,
205:             # never a measured zero, including individual task/trial score fields.
206:             withheld = [dict(row, status="unmeasured", score=None,
207:                              reason="score withheld: cleanup and snapshot lifecycle publication gate pending")
208:                         if row["status"] == "measured" else row for row in summary["outcomes"]]
209:             receipt.update(measurement_summary(selected, withheld))
210:             receipt["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] = len(set(eligible))
211:         else:
212:             _require_publication(receipt, summary)
213:     return receipt
214: 
288: def require_review(bundle: dict, *, context: dict, eligible: list[str], run_id: str | None = None) -> None:
289:     """Expansion requires a distinct, hash-bound review of a complete diagnostic."""
290:     _require(isinstance(bundle, dict), "diagnostic review must be an object")
291:     receipt = bundle.get("receipt")
292:     validate_receipt(receipt)
293:     _require("enforcement" not in receipt,
294:              "unobserved diagnostic cannot authorize expansion")
295:     _require(receipt["stage"] == "diagnostic", "expansion requires a diagnostic receipt")
296:     _require(receipt["coverage"]["eligible_tasks"] == len(set(eligible))
297:              and receipt["eligible_task_ids_sha256"] == eligible_digest(eligible)
298:              and receipt["selected_task_ids"] == diagnostic_task_set(eligible),
299:              "diagnostic does not match the current eligible pool")
300:     validate_context(context)
301:     for key in ("dataset", "side", "metric", "hashes", "endpoint", "limits", "gates"):
302:         _require(receipt["context"][key] == context[key], f"diagnostic context changed: {key}")
303:     _require(all(_sha(context["hashes"][k]) for k in HASH_NAMES)
304:              and _sha(context["metric"]["scorer_sha256"]) and context["endpoint"] in ENDPOINTS
305:              and all(context["limits"][k] is not None for k in ("resource", "budget")),
306:              "unknown provenance, endpoint or limits cannot authorize expansion")
307:     _require(context["gates"]["status"] == "passed" and all(
308:         receipt[k]["status"] == "passed" for k in ("cleanup", "snapshot")),
309:         "gates, cleanup and snapshot must have passing evidence")
310:     _require(receipt["coverage"]["measured_trials"] == 1
311:              and receipt["coverage"]["invalid_trials"] == 0
312:              and receipt["coverage"]["unmeasured_trials"] == 0,
313:              "expansion requires a valid measured diagnostic")
314:     review = bundle.get("review")
315:     _require(isinstance(review, dict) and review.get("approved") is True
316:              and _text(review.get("reviewer")) and _text(review.get("reviewed_at"))
317:              and review.get("receipt_sha256") == receipt_digest(receipt),
318:              "separate approved review must bind the exact diagnostic receipt")
319:     _require(run_id is None or run_id != receipt["run_id"], "expansion requires a fresh run ID")

eval/admission/lifecycle_receipt.py
127: def _advance(receipt, stage, evidence):
128:     _require(stage in STAGES[1:], "unknown lifecycle stage")
129:     _require(receipt["state"] != "cleanup", "cleanup is terminal; use a fresh run ID")
130:     if receipt["status"] != "passed":
131:         _require(stage == "cleanup", "failed lifecycle may only attempt cleanup")
132:     else:
133:         _require(stage == STAGES[STAGES.index(receipt["state"]) + 1],
134:                  "lifecycle stage skipped or repeated")
135:     if stage in ("admit", "cleanup"):
136:         checked = _gate(receipt["binding"], "runtime" if stage == "admit" else stage, evidence)
137:         status = "passed" if checked["status"] == "passed" else "failed"
138:         if status == "failed":
139:             evidence = {"refusal": checked}
140:     else:
141:         checked = _attestation(receipt["binding"], stage, evidence)
142:         status = checked["status"]
143:     if stage == "seal" and status == "passed":
144:         scored = receipt["events"][-1]["evidence"]["facts"]["outcomes_sha256"]
145:         _require(checked["facts"]["outcomes_sha256"] == scored, "sealed outcomes differ from scoring")
146:     result = copy.deepcopy(receipt)
147:     result["events"].append(dict(stage=stage, status=status,
148:                                  evidence=copy.deepcopy(evidence), receipt_sha256=digest(checked)))
149:     result["state"] = stage
150:     # Failed/unknown work remains terminal for scoring even after successful cleanup.
151:     result["status"] = status if receipt["status"] == "passed" else receipt["status"]
152:     result["publication_ready"] = stage == "cleanup" and result["status"] == "passed"
153:     _bounded(result)
181: def require_scope(receipt: dict, *, run_id: str, manifest_sha256: str, scope_sha256: str) -> None:
182:     """Check even incomplete checkpoints against their frozen publication scope."""
183:     validate_lifecycle_receipt(receipt)
184:     _require(receipt["binding"]["run_id"] == run_id
185:              and receipt["binding"]["manifest_sha256"] == manifest_sha256
186:              and receipt["scope_sha256"] == scope_sha256, "publication scope/binding changed")
187: 
188: 
189: def require_publication(receipt: dict, *, run_id: str, manifest_sha256: str,
190:                         scope_sha256: str, outcomes_sha256: str,
191:                         cleanup_sha256: str, snapshot_sha256: str) -> None:
192:     """Permit structural publication only for this exact scope and sealed result."""
193:     require_scope(receipt, run_id=run_id, manifest_sha256=manifest_sha256, scope_sha256=scope_sha256)
194:     _require(receipt["publication_ready"], "cleanup and snapshot lifecycle must pass before publication")
195:     seal, cleanup = receipt["events"][-2:]
196:     facts = seal["evidence"]["facts"]
197:     _require(facts["outcomes_sha256"] == outcomes_sha256, "publication outcomes changed after seal")
198:     _require(cleanup["receipt_sha256"] == cleanup_sha256
199:              and digest(facts["snapshot"]) == snapshot_sha256,
200:              "publication cleanup and snapshot attestations differ")

eval/run/runner.py
39: def _refuse_unobserved_policies(task: dict, env: dict | None,
40:                                verifier: dict | None, *, run_id: str = "",
41:                                diagnostic_authorization: Authorization | None = None) -> dict | None:
42:     """Refuse policy-bearing tasks until a trusted backend observer is wired in.
43: 
44:     The offline runner_admission checks cannot discover enforcement. Launch flags,
45:     adapter observations and cached verdicts are not a substitute for that observer.
46:     An operator may authorize one explicitly unmeasured diagnostic attempt. That
47:     permission is never treated as backend observation or production admission.
48:     """
49:     env_spec = env or {}
50:     fields = {"env." + key for key in (
51:         "cpus", "memory_mb", "agent_timeout_s", "verify_timeout_s", "network",
52:         "resources", "filesystems", "resource_policy", "network_policy", "artifact_policy",
53:     ) if key in env_spec}
54:     # Even direct callers that omit normalized fields request these default bounds
55:     # by choosing exec. The registry normally fills them before reaching the runner.
56:     if env_spec.get("kind") == "exec":
57:         fields.add("env.exec_defaults")
58:     if env_spec.get("state") == "container":
59:         fields.add("env.state=container")
60:     # `verifier.timeout_s` is deliberately NOT a trigger. The platform enforces it
61:     # itself -- `eval/verification/verifier.py` passes it to `phase_timeout` and to the verifier
62:     # subprocess' `timeout=`, and records expiry as its own outcome ("the verifier
63:     # timed out after Ns"), which is an unmeasured result rather than a score. There
64:     # is no external backend capability to observe, so demanding an observation for it
65:     # refused every dataset that declares one (the shared QA scaffold sets 3600s for
66:     # all of its adapters) while adding no guarantee. Everything whose enforcement
67:     # really does live outside this process -- exec defaults, container state, network,
68:     # cpus/memory, filesystems, resource and artifact policy -- still refuses.
69:     if "artifact_policy" in (verifier or {}):
70:         fields.add("verifier.artifact_policy")
71:     if not fields:
72:         return None
73:     if diagnostic_authorization is not None:
74:         # Still refuse every ordinary run, pilot, unbound task/run and cached
75:         # measurement. Only the bound one-task diagnostic may attempt execution;
76:         # its caller records non-observation and forbids comparable publication.
77:         diagnostic_authorization.require_scope(run_id, [task["task_id"]])
78:         return None
79:     result = _invalid(task, "backend_observation_unavailable", stage="admission", service=None)
80:     result["invalid"]["receipt"] = {
81:         "schema": "harnessgrad.runner-policy-refusal.v1", "stage": "admission",
82:         "status": "blocked", "reason": "backend_observation_unavailable",
83:         "measured": False, "production_authorized": False, "observation": "unknown",
84:         "policy_fields": sorted(fields),
85:     }
86:     return result
154:             verify_timeout_s: int | None = None,
155:             env: dict | None = None,
156:             run_id: str = "", run_seed: str = "",
157:             recordings_root: Path | None = None,
158:             diagnostic_authorization: Authorization | None = None,
159:             trial_id: str | None = None) -> dict:
160:     if diagnostic_authorization is not None:
161:         diagnostic_authorization.require_scope(run_id, [task["task_id"]])
162:     refusal = _refuse_unobserved_policies(
163:         task, env, verifier, run_id=run_id,
164:         diagnostic_authorization=diagnostic_authorization)
165:     result = refusal if refusal is not None else _run_one(
166:         repo, task, timeout_s, sandbox, setup, verifier, verify_timeout_s,
167:         env, run_id, run_seed, recordings_root, trial_id=trial_id)
168:     if result.get("verdict") is not None:
169:         result["verdict"].setdefault("scorer_isolation_class", "unknown")
170:     if diagnostic_authorization is not None:
171:         result["enforcement"] = diagnostic_authorization.record()
172:     return result

docs/enforcement-proof-decision.md
27: ## What is still done, and must not be read as withdrawn
28: 
29: Withdrawing the *proof* requirement is not withdrawing the *behaviour*:
30: 
31: - scrapers and verifiers still rematerialize inputs, still snapshot and still clean up.
32:   `eval/admission/runner_admission.py::admit_snapshot` and `admit_cleanup` check available
33:   observations **best-effort, with uncertainty retained**. Missing or null facts, missing
34:   owned-object observations and incomplete namespace coverage do not by themselves refuse
35:   admission or quarantine a lease. Passing receipts explicitly record `best_effort=true`
36:   and `uncertainties`; they do not certify bounded peak snapshot usage, exact owned absence
37:   or atomic lease closure. A known failure or mismatch still refuses admission and is
38:   recorded in a blocked receipt; cleanup refusals still request `lease_action=quarantine`.
39:   Cleanup can request `lease_action=release` with recorded uncertainty. These functions
40:   inspect facts; they do not replace snapshot/cleanup execution or ledger persistence;
41: - each dataset review still records **metric identity** — what the number is and where it
42:   comes from — because that is what the reviews are for, and `official-scorer-identity`'s own
43:   `next_action` asked for documentation, not enforcement. The documentation continues; the
44:   blocker that gated the datasets on *proof* does not;
45: - the realtime refusal contract is untouched: a declared policy that needs a backend
46:   observation this checkout cannot supply still refuses before staging.
47: 
48: The snapshot/cleanup change preserves binding and input integrity: stale run/trial/manifest
49: bindings, malformed values, unexpected identities and invalid supplied hashes still refuse.
50: Omitted/null lifecycle facts, owned-object identity components and evidence hashes record
51: uncertainty; they are not invented successful observations. Known identity components must
52: still match the owned policy. An observed nonzero exit, OOM, running worker, deadline mismatch,
53: size/headroom violation, surviving owned object or nonempty owned namespace is still bad
54: even when other observations are missing. A passing admission receipt remains unmeasured
55: and grants no production authorization. Snapshot/cleanup uncertainty alone is not a new
[exit=0]

$ cat /tmp/w-s6-preservation.py
import copy,json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def original(path):
 return subprocess.check_output(['git','show',f'HEAD:{path}'])
path='docs/dataset-blockers.json'
before=json.loads(original(path))
after=json.loads(Path(path).read_text())
old=next(row for row in before['shared_blockers'] if row['id']=='scorer-offline-closure')
new=next(row for row in after['shared_blockers'] if row['id']=='scorer-offline-closure')
assert new['observed'].startswith(old['observed']+' Standing structural note - w-s6:')
assert new['evidence'][:-1]==old['evidence']
for field in old:
 if field not in ('observed','evidence'):assert new[field]==old[field],field
expected=copy.deepcopy(before)
target=next(row for row in expected['shared_blockers'] if row['id']=='scorer-offline-closure')
target['observed']=new['observed']
target['evidence']=new['evidence']
assert after==expected
for item in new['evidence']:
 if item['kind']=='repository':assert item['locator'] in Path(item['path']).read_text(),item
print('Registry delta is exactly scorer-offline-closure observed append plus one evidence entry.')
print('All statuses/next_actions, other shared data, dataset blockers/references, review and historical records equal HEAD.')
print('All scorer evidence locators appear verbatim in their referenced repository files.')
journal='docs/adapter-integration-journal.md'
assert Path(journal).read_bytes().startswith(original(journal))
headings=[line for line in Path(journal).read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith('#') and line.endswith(' - w-s6')]
assert len(headings)==1
print('Journal is append-only; unique heading:',headings[0])
paths=[f'reports/w-{name}-evidence/execution/diagnostic_receipt.json' for name in ('alf','net','chess3','appworld')]
paths+=['tests/fixtures/hg_wpristine/evidence/measurement-audit.json','reports/w-prifix-evidence/final-control/control-receipt.json']
for path in paths:
 assert Path(path).read_bytes()==original(path),path
 print('Existing receipt byte-identical to HEAD:',path)
changed=subprocess.check_output(['git','diff','--name-only','HEAD'],text=True).splitlines()
assert set(changed)<= {'docs/dataset-blockers.json','docs/adapter-integration-journal.md'}
print('Tracked changed paths:',json.dumps(changed))
assert subprocess.check_output(['git','branch','--show-current'],text=True).strip()=='wg-s6'
print('Commit target is wg-s6 in this isolated worktree; source, runs and frozen receipts have no tracked diff.')

import copy,json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def original(path):
 return subprocess.check_output(['git','show',f'HEAD:{path}'])
path='docs/dataset-blockers.json'
before=json.loads(original(path))
after=json.loads(Path(path).read_text())
old=next(row for row in before['shared_blockers'] if row['id']=='scorer-offline-closure')
new=next(row for row in after['shared_blockers'] if row['id']=='scorer-offline-closure')
assert new['observed'].startswith(old['observed']+' Standing structural note - w-s6:')
assert new['evidence'][:-1]==old['evidence']
for field in old:
 if field not in ('observed','evidence'):assert new[field]==old[field],field
expected=copy.deepcopy(before)
target=next(row for row in expected['shared_blockers'] if row['id']=='scorer-offline-closure')
target['observed']=new['observed']
target['evidence']=new['evidence']
assert after==expected
for item in new['evidence']:
 if item['kind']=='repository':assert item['locator'] in Path(item['path']).read_text(),item
print('Registry delta is exactly scorer-offline-closure observed append plus one evidence entry.')
print('All statuses/next_actions, other shared data, dataset blockers/references, review and historical records equal HEAD.')
print('All scorer evidence locators appear verbatim in their referenced repository files.')
journal='docs/adapter-integration-journal.md'
assert Path(journal).read_bytes().startswith(original(journal))
headings=[line for line in Path(journal).read_text().splitlines() if line.startswith('#') and line.endswith(' - w-s6')]
assert len(headings)==1
print('Journal is append-only; unique heading:',headings[0])
paths=[f'reports/w-{name}-evidence/execution/diagnostic_receipt.json' for name in ('alf','net','chess3','appworld')]
paths+=['tests/fixtures/hg_wpristine/evidence/measurement-audit.json','reports/w-prifix-evidence/final-control/control-receipt.json']
for path in paths:
 assert Path(path).read_bytes()==original(path),path
 print('Existing receipt byte-identical to HEAD:',path)
changed=subprocess.check_output(['git','diff','--name-only','HEAD'],text=True).splitlines()
assert set(changed)<= {'docs/dataset-blockers.json','docs/adapter-integration-journal.md','reports/w-s6-determination.md','reports/w-s6-validation.txt'}
print('Tracked changed paths:',json.dumps(changed))
assert subprocess.check_output(['git','branch','--show-current'],text=True).strip()=='wg-s6'
print('Commit target is wg-s6 in this isolated worktree; source, runs and frozen receipts have no tracked diff.')
